patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippa8ḷar.

vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.


cāṉṟitaḻ
paṇṭita ñā. tēvanēyaṉār, pi.ō.el. potuvāka moḻinūl āyvu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻccol ārāyccipaṟṟi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭakālat tēviyiṉi niṟivu ceytaṉa. tammuṭiya iḷantik kālattilē talimik kaṇkāṇiyār tavattiru tirañcu (Trench) eḻutiya collārāycci, pērāciriyar mākkacu mullar eḻutiya moḻiyaṟiviyal, pērāciriyar cāycu eḻutiya oppiyal moḻinūl mutaliya āṅkiya nūlkaḷi yāmē peruviruppuṭaṉ paṭittukkoṇṭirukkumpōtu anta muṟiyil tamiḻc coṟkaḷi ārāya vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉēm. tamiḻp pēraṟiñarkaṭkup pulappaṭāmal maṟintu kiṭanta virivākavum viyappākavumuḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivup parappu, paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel eḻutiya tirāviṭa moḻi
kaḷiṉ oppiyal āyvu nūlāl pulappaṭalāyiṟṟu. eṉiṉum paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel, aṟiyappaṭāta vaṭṭārattil ceyta
toru muyaṟciyātalāl tamiḻccoṟkaḷi yellām viṭāmal niṟivāka eṭuttārāyntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu etirpārppataṟ
killi. ituvē moḻiyiyali ārāya vēṇṭu meṉṟu emmit tūṇṭiyatu. eṉavē `ñāṉacākaram (aṟivukkaṭal) eṉṉum emmuṭiya itaḻiṉ mutal tokutiyil at tuṟiyil oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēm. āṉāl appōtu camayam, meypporuḷiyal, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiya tuṟikaḷil emmuṭiya moḻiyārāyccit tuṟiyil toṭarntu īṭupaṭak kūṭavilli. āyiṉum takutiyuṭiya aṟiñar yārāvatu it tuṟiyil ārāyvataṟku muṉvarakkūṭumā eṉṟu etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntēm. appōtu yāḻppāṇam tiruttanti ñāṉappirakācartām ekipatiya moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūli emakku aṉuppivittār.  atu ōraḷavu emakku maṉaniṟivu aḷittatu. eṉiṉum moḻiyiyal ārāyccit
tuṟi mikavum virivum āḻamu muṭiyatātalāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟṟu avaṟṟik koṇṭu naṉku purintukoḷḷum vakiyil mēlum moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷivarutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉēm. anta nērattil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār yām etirpārttati ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum niṟivēṟṟiyatu kaṇṭu perumakiḻvuṟṟēm. at tuṟiyil avar mikavum uḻippeṭuttu ārāyntu eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟit tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ naṉṟāka nampalām.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār oppaṟṟa taṉit tiṟamiyuṭiyavar eṉṟum, avarukku oppāka iruppavar arumiyākum eṉṟum yām uṇmiyākavē karutukiṉṟēm.
mēlum tiru. tēvanēyaṉār patiyac collum āciriyarum iṉpuṟuttum coṟpoḻivāḷarumāvar. pala tamiḻk kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻākkaḷil ematu talimiyiṉkīḻ avar coṟ
poḻivukaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷār. avi kēṭṭārp piṇikkun takiyavāy amintu aviyōrik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceytu makiḻccik kaṭalil āḻttiṉa. mt® tUªâíiH¤J MuhŒ¢á brŒJtU« m¿P® Mjyhš mtiu¥ gÂÆš mk®¤J« vªj Ãiya¤J¡F« mtuhš ngU« òfG« »il¡f¥

- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
*  maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṅkilattil eḻutiya cāṉṟitaḻiṉ tamiḻākkam.

mukavuri
ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ talimiyāṉavaṟṟuḷ tamiḻum oṉṟeṉiṉum, palkuḻuvum pāḻceyum uṭpakiyum tamiḻakattiliruntukoṇṭu, tamiḻiṉ perumiyip piṟanāṭukaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻnāṭum aṟiyātapaṭi, ataṉi maṟittu varuvatu mika mika iraṅkattakkatoṉṟām. ārāycciyāḷarō veṉiṉ, ōriruvar nīṅkalākap piṟarellām, piṟanāṭṭuc ceytikaḷāyiṉ maṟinta vuṇmiyi veḷippaṭuttuvatum, tamiḻnāṭṭuc ceytikaḷāyiṉ veḷippaṭṭa vuṇmiyi maṟittu vippatumē toḻilākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. āyiṉum, kār kālattil maṟikkappaṭṭa katiravaṉ tiṭumeṉa orunāḷ tikaḻntu tōṉṟuvatupōl, tamiḻum orunāḷ ulakattiṟku veḷippaṭum eṉpataṟku eṭṭuṇiyum aiyamiṉṟu.
tamiḻē tiraviṭattāy eṉpatu mikat teḷivāyiruppiṉum, 1891ām āṇṭilēyē,
kaṉṉaṭamum kaḷiteluṅkum kaviṉmaliyā ḷamuntuḷuvum 
uṉṉutarat tutitteḻuntē oṉṟupala āyiṭiṉum
eṉṟu pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ tiṭṭamāyk kūṟiyiruppavum, ārāycciyiṉmiyālō, kavaliyiṉmiyālō, tuṇiviṉmiyālō, tamiḻppulavar eṭuttuk kāṭṭātatiṉāl, tamiḻiṉ tiraviṭattāymi potumakkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭātiruppatuṭaṉ, tamiḻ oru puṉciṟu putu moḻiyiṉum tāḻvākak karutappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
peluccittāṉattilum vaṭintiyāvilum iṉṉum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatiyum, vaṭanāṭṭu āriya moḻikaḷilum tiraviṭa neṟimuṟikaḷē aṭippaṭiyāy amintu kiṭappatiyum, kuccaramum (kucarātti), marāṭṭiyum paṇṭik kālattil pañcat tirāviṭikaḷil iraṇṭāka vaṭamoḻiyāḷarālēyē koḷḷappaṭṭatiyum, intiya moḻikaḷilellām mūviṭappeyarkaḷum mukkiyamāṉa muṟippeyarkaḷum tamiḻc coṟkaḷāy allatu tamiḻ vēraṭip piṟantaṉavāyiruppatiyum, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil teluṅku nāṭṭiṟkum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟkum ceṉṟa tamiḻk kuṟavar (eṟukkalavāru allatu koravaru) kūṭa, iṉṟu tamiḻit teluṅkum kaṉṉaṭamum pōla olittup pēcuvatiyum, nōkkumiṭattut tamiḻiṟ puṇarcciyum parucoṉṉiliyum tōṉṟāta toṉmutu kālattil, tamiḻē intiyā muḻutum taṉip pērāṭci peṟṟiruntami pulaṉām.
taṭpa veppa niliyiṉālum, olimuṟic cōmpaliṉālum, ilakkiya vilakkaṇa aṇikari yiṉmiyālum, āriyak kalappiṉālum, tamiḻar viḻippiṉmiyālum, nāvalan tīvu muḻuvatum orutaṉiyāy vaḻaṅkiya mutupaḻantamiḻ, palvēṟu moḻikaḷāyp pirintu, vaṭanāṭṭil āriyamayamāyum teṉṉāṭṭil āriyak kalappiṉatāyum vēṟupaṭṭatuṭaṉ, āriyaccārpu mikka tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ mēṉmēlum tamiḻi nerukki nerukkit teṟkē taḷḷikkoṇṭē varukiṉṟaṉa.
neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamum toṭiyōḷ puvamun
  tamiḻvaram paṟutta taṇpuṉal naṉṉāṭṭu
eṉṉum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟṟāl, ki.pi.2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari vēṅkaṭatti vaṭavelliyākak koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu muḻutum piṟamoḻi vaḻaṅkāta tamiḻnāṭāyiruntami pulaṉām. āṉāl, 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil cēranāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattiṟ kaṉṉaṭam pukuntuviṭṭatu. iti,
veḷḷā ṟatuvaṭakkā mēṟkup peruvaḻiyām
 teḷḷār puṉaṟkaṉṉi teṟkākum - uḷḷāra
 āṇṭa kaṭalkiḻakkā mimpat taṟukātam
 pāṇṭināṭ ṭellip pati.
kaṭalkiḻakkut teṟkuk kariporuveḷ ḷāṟu
 kuṭaticiyiṟ kōṭṭik kariyām - vaṭaticiyil
 ēṇāṭṭup paṇṇi yirupattu nāṟkātam
 cōṇāṭṭuk kelliyeṉac col.
mēṟkup pavaḷamali vēṅkaṭa nērvaṭakkām
 ārkku muvari yaṇikiḻakku - pārkkuḷuyar
 teṟkup piṉāki tikaḻirupa tiṉkātam
 naṟṟoṇṭi nāṭeṉavē nāṭṭu.
vaṭakkut ticipaḻaṉi vāṉkīḻteṉ kāci
 kuṭakkut ticikōḻik kōṭām - kaṭaṟkariyiṉ
 ōramō teṟkāku muḷḷeṇ patiṉkātam
 cēranāṭ ṭelliyeṉac ceppu
eṉṉum kampar ceyyuṭkaḷāṉaṟika.
iṟṟiniliyilō, cēranāṭṭiṉ mēlpākam muḻutum maliyāḷa nāṭāyum kīḻpākattiṉ vaṭapakuti kaṉṉaṭa nāṭāyum māṟiyiruppatuṭaṉ cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭamuṉip pakuti (toṇṭi nāṭṭup palkuṉṟak kōṭṭam) teluṅkiṟ kiṭantantu irumoḻi nāṭāy vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu.
uṇmiyil oru perumiyumillāta puṉciṟu putu moḻikaḷi yellām avvam moḻiyār palapaṭap pārāṭṭi vaḷarttu varukiyil, pala vakiyil taliciṟanta taṉipperun tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻit tamiḻar nekiḻaviṭuvatum, eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟemak keṉṟiruppatum, tamiḻap piṟappiṟku muṟṟum takāta ceytiyām.
tamiḻiyum piṟa tiraviṭamoḻikaḷiyum ārāyvatāl tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyum paḻantamiḻariṉ perumiyiyu maṟivatuṭaṉ, ulaka muḻutun taḻīiya kulanūl (Ethnology), varalāṟṟunūl (History), moḻinūl (Philology) ākiya mukkali kaḷiṉ tiṟavukōliyum kāṇap peṟuvatāyiruttaliṉ, iṉimēlāyiṉun tamiḻar tam kaṭami yuṇarntu kaṭippiṭippārāka.
eṉatu oppiyaṉ moḻi nūliṉ mutaṉ maṭal 3ām pākattiṉ piṟpakuti yāy veḷivarum in nūl, tamiḻē tiraviṭat tāy eṉṟu nāṭṭa veḻuntatu. iti naṭuvuniliyāyp paṭippārkkellām ivvuṇmi pulaṉākumeṉpatu tiṇṇam. oru moḻikku aṭippaṭiyāṉavum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavumāṉa, mūviṭap peyarkaḷ, muṟip peyarkaḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭup peyarkaḷ, akkampakkap poruṭ peyarkaḷ, pēriṭap peyarkaḷ, vā, pō mutaliya mukkiya viṉikaḷ, palvakip paṇpuppeyarkaḷ, ki kāl mutaliya ciṉippeyarkaḷ ākiya ivi, iyaṟkiyāṉa vaṭivilum vērpporuḷ tāṅkiyum emmoḻiyiluḷḷaṉavō, am moḻiyē ataṟkut tāy eṉat tuṇital vēṇṭum.
āriyaveḻuttuppōl eṭuttum urappiyum olikkum mūcceḻuttukaḷum ōciyeḻuttukaḷum (Aspirated and voiced letters) tamiḻukkiṉmiyiṉ tamiḻukkuriya potu veḻuttukaḷālēyē perumpālum piṟa tiraviṭaccoṟkaḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷiyellām eṭutteḻutiṉ pala akarātikaḷāka viriyumātaliṉ, eṭuttukkāṭṭukku vēṇṭiya aḷavāṉa coṟkaḷē iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉa aṟika.
in nūliṉ tiruttampaṟṟiya karuttukaḷi aṟiñar terivippiṉ, avaṟṟi naṉṟiyaṟivuṭaṉ ēṟṟukkoṇṭu, aṭutta patippukaḷiṟ payaṉpaṭuttum vāyppuṭi yēṉāvēṉ.
kuṇanāṭik kuṟṟamu nāṭi yavaṟṟuḷ
   mikināṭi mikka koḷal.
   kuṟṟaṅ kaḷintu kuṟipeytu vācittal
   kaṟṟaṟinta māntar kaṭaṉ.

tiraviṭattāy - 1



muṉṉuri
1. moḻi eṉṟāl eṉṉa?
oru vakuppār allatu nāṭṭār tam karuttip piṟarkkup pulappaṭuttaṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷum olittokutiyē moḻiyām. av voli bcālluñbcāṟṟoṭarumāyirukkum.brhšY« ōreḻuttuc col īreḻuttac col mutaliyavākap palavakittu,
ōr oli vēṟu; ataṉāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷvēṟu. olikkum poruḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu varivaṭi veḻuttiṟkum olivaṭi veḻuttiṟkum uḷḷatē. kāṟṟu iyaṅkuvatiṉālum iru allatu pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu urāyvataṉālum ōci piṟakkum. av vōcikku iyalpāyp poruḷilli. av vōciyip poruḷuṇarttum aṭiyāḷamākak koṇṭatē moḻiyām. kāṟṟiyakkam veḷḷiṭiyiyaṅkuvatum uyiri (pirāṇi)kaḷiṉ vāyvaḻi iyaṅkuvatum eṉa iruvakittu. ivaṟṟuḷ, piṉṉataṉ payaṉāya olikaḷē perumpālum moḻik karuviyām. olikku iyalpāyp poruḷuṇarttum taṉmi yiruppiṉ, ulakameṅkum eṉṟum oru moḻiyāyum atuvum kallāmalē aṟiyappaṭuvatāyu mirukkum. aṅṅaṉam iṉmiyaṟika.
moḻinūliṉ tiṟamaṟiyāta cilar, ōci nilippāṉatu ākavē ōcivaṭivāṉa moḻikaḷum nilippāṉavi eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu, irumpu nilippāṉatu; ākavē, irumpu vaṭivāṉa iyantiraṅkaḷum nilippāṉavi; putitāyuṇṭāyaviyalla eṉṟu kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatē.
ovvoru  nāṭṭārum  perumpālum  vevvēṟu moḻiyip  pēcivarukiṉṟaṉar.  oru nāṭṭār  maṟṟoru        nāṭṭāriṉ moḻiyik kaṟṟāloḻiyap pēca muṭiyātu. oru nāṭṭāruḷḷum kuḻantikaḷ periyōr vāyilāyk kēṭṭāloḻiyat tam tāy moḻiyip pēcamuṭiyātu. kuḻavip paruvattilēyē tam nāṭṭāriṉiṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭu moḻi vaḻaṅkāta allatu tāymoḻi vaḻaṅkāta iṭattil niliyāy vāḻntavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyip pēca muṭiyātu. muḻuc ceviṭar piṟar pēcuvatik kēṭka iyalāmiyālēyē perumpālum ūmiyarākiṉṟaṉar. vilaṅkukaḷiyum paṟavikaḷiyum pōṉṟu, mikac cila olikaḷāl mikac cila karuttukaḷit terivittuk koṇṭa tamakkeṉa moḻiyillāta anākarika makkaḷum cila kāṭukaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru nāṭṭilum makkaḷiṉ nākarika vaḷarccikkēṟpa moḻiyum vaḷarntuḷḷatu.
oruvaṉukkup piṟappilēyē tāymoḻi yaṟivilli; atip pēcuntiṟaṉ allatu kaṟkuntiṟaṉ maṭṭumuṇṭu. ōrāṇṭuk kuḻanti ammā, appā, pācci (pāl), cōcci (cōṟu)  mutaliya peyarkaḷiyum, oṉṟariyāṇṭuk kuḻanti avaṟṟoṭu vā, pō mutaliya viṉikaḷiyum, īrāṇṭuk kuḻanti taṉ karuttiṟkuriya coṟkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum collak kaṟṟukkoḷkiṟatu. allatu kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟatu. mūvāṇṭuk kuḻanti naṉṟāyp pēcukiṟatu; piṉpu vīṭṭiviṭṭu veḷiyēṟip poruḷkaḷiyum ceytikaḷiyum aṟiyaaṟiyac coṟṟokuti yum peruki varukiṟatu; ataṉpiṉ kalviyiṉāl pala coṟkaḷ putitāy aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam oruvaṉ vaḷara vaḷara, aṟivu perukap peruka, taṉ coṟṟokutiyi mikuttuk koṇṭē pōkiṉṟāṉ. oru kuḻanti taṉ peṟṟōriṭat tiṉiṉṟu pēcak kaṟṟukkoṇṭāṟpōla, oru nāṭṭār tam muṉṉōriṭattiṉiṉṟu pēcak kaṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
mēṟkūṟiya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, māntaṉ oru kālattil moḻiyillātiruntāṉ eṉpatum, moḻiyaminta piṉ vaḻivaḻip piṉṉōrellām muṉṉōriṭattiṉiṉṟu moḻiyik kaṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatum uyttuṇarappaṭum.
ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷellām ōriṭattiṉiṉṟē paravip pōyiṉar  eṉpatum, oru  tāyvayiṟṟiṉarē eṉpatum       ārāycciyāṟ kaṇṭa muṭipukaḷām. māntaṉ mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya iṭattil makkaḷ moḻiyillāmalē cilakālam vāḻntu vantaṉar. moḻiyillāta niliyil cila muṟiyum moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ cila muṟiyumāka makkaḷ palaticikkum pala muṟi paravip pōyirukkiṉṟaṉar. moḻiyillātu pirintupōṉavar tām pōṉa iṭaṅkaḷil putitāyt tattamakku ēṟṟavāṟum iyaṉṟavāṟum iyaṉmoḻikaḷi ākkikkoṇṭaṉar. moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ pirintu pōṉavar tattam cuṟṟuc cārpiṟkēṟpap putuc coṟkaḷi amittum taṭpaveppa nilikkēṟpap paḻañ coṟkaḷit tirittuṅ koṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, toṭarpuṭiya makkaḷellām toṭarpuṭiya moḻikaḷip pēci varuvatum, makkaḷ toṭarpiṉ perumi ciṟumikkēṟpa avarkaḷ moḻikaḷiṉ toṭarpum mikkum kuṟintu miruppatum iyalpām. itaṟku, amerikka nīkirōvip pōla anākarika moḻiyarāyum kōvāp pōrttukkīciyarip pōlac ciṟupāṉmiyarāyumiruntu, ayaṉmoḻikaḷik kaṭippiṭippatu vilakkām. vēṟu cila vilakkukaḷu muḷa; avi vēṇṭumiṭattuk kūṟappaṭum.
2. tamiḻmoḻi etu?
paṉimali tōṉṟāta mutaṟkālattil nāvalantēya muḻutum, tolkāppiyar kālattil vēṅkaṭattiṟkum (teṟkē yamiḻntupōṉa) kumarimalikkum iṭiyilum vaḻaṅkivantu, taṟpōtu vēṅkaṭattiṟkum kumarimuṉikkum iṭiyil mēṟkut toṭarcci malikkum micūrc cīmikkum kiḻakkil vaḻaṅkivaruvatu tamiḻmoḻiyām.
3. tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar
tamiḻ eṉṉum peyarkkup poruttamāṉa poruḷkaḷ taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭatu, (2) taṉimi eṉa iraṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu poruḷāyiṉ ulakil piṟamoḻi allatu moḻikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa allatu tirinta piṉṉum tamiḻil ḻakaran tōṉṟiṉa piṉṉum tamiḻukkup peyar ēṟpaṭṭiruttalvēṇṭum. piṉṉatu poruḷāyiṉ tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar mutalilēyē tamiḻiyō mutalāvatu tamiḻarkkup peyarākip piṉpu tamiḻiyō kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum. ituvum piṟanāṭum piṟamoḻiyum       tōṉṟiya piṉṉarē nikaḻntiruttal kūṭum. pala moḻi vaḻaṅkāta pōtu oru moḻikkumaṭṭum ciṟappup peyar amital aritu. iv viyalpu piṟa poruḷkaṭkum ēṟkum. taṉimi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum  tamiḻ eṉṉuñ col mutalāvatu tamiḻarik kuṟittiruppatu avariṉ taṉippaṭṭa nākarikampaṟṟi. paṇṭik kālattil nāvalantēyattil (intiyāvil) maṭṭumalla, ulaka muḻuvatilumē tamiḻar taliciṟanta nākarikarāyiruntatiṉāl taṉippaṭṭavar eṉṉum poruḷil tamiḻar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. paḻantamiḻariṉ nākarikatti, avaruṭiya paḻakkavaḻakkam, ciṟanta karuttukaḷ, ceyyuḷvaṉmi, avar ceytirunta poṟikaḷ, tamiḻiṉ amiti, muttamiḻp puṇarppu, tamiḻkkali nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyalām. avaratu ciṟanta oḻukkattiṉālēyē,
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
 āyiṭit
 tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu
eṉap paṇṭit tamiḻakattip pārāṭṭik kūṟiṉar paṉam pāraṉārum.
nāṭā koṉṟō kāṭā koṉṟō
 avalā koṉṟō miciyā koṉṟō
 evvaḻi nallavar āṭavar
 avvaḻi nalli vāḻiya nilaṉē	(puṟam. 187)
eṉṟār auviyārum.
oru kulattāriṉ allatu nāṭṭāriṉ peyar tirintum tiriyātum avaratu moḻiyik kuṟippatu iyalpē. āṅkilaratu (Angles) moḻi āṅkilam (English) eṉṟum, cerumaṉiyaratu (Germans) moḻi cerumaṉiyam (German) eṉṟum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
tamiḻ eṉṉum col taṉimi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum pōtu, ataṉ ḻakaram, amiḻ imiḻ umiḻ kumiḻ eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōla oru vikutiyām. 
tamiḻ iṉimiyā yiruttaliṉ, tamiḻ eṉṉuñ collukku iṉimip poruḷ kūṟuvar. itu vaḻipporuḷēyaṉṟi vērpporuḷaṉṟu.
4. kumari nāṭē (Lemuria) tamiḻ (tiraviṭam) tōṉṟiya iṭam
ulakil itupōtu tiraviṭamiruppatu intiyāvē; intiyāvilum tiraviṭattiṟkuc ciṟantatu teṉṉāṭē; teṉ ṉāṭṭilum ciṟantatu tamiḻnāṭē; tamiḻnāṭṭilum moḻit tiruttam teṟku nōkkiyatē.
muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷil pāṇṭi nāṭṭiyē tamiḻ nāṭeṉṟum, muttamiḻaracaruḷ pāṇṭiyaṉiyē tamiḻnāṭaṉ eṉṟum, paṇṭik kālattilēyē ciṟappittuk kūṟiṉar. iṉṟum pāṇṭināṭ ṭelliyiltāṉ tamiḻiṉ vaḷattiyum tūymiyi yum oru ciṟitu kāṇalām.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ perumpakuti īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē ciṟitu ciṟitākat teṉperuṅ kaṭalil mūḻkiviṭṭatu. pāṇṭiyar irīiya mukkaḻakaṅ (muccaṅkaṅ)kaḷum nīṭitta kālattaṉa; neṭṭiṭiyiṭṭaṉa.
iṟiyaṉārakapporuḷuriyil mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟil,

vaḻaṅkuva tuḷvīḻntak kaṇṇum paḻaṅkuṭi 
 paṇpil talippirita liṉṟu	(kuṟaḷ. 955)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷuriyil, paḻaṅkuṭikku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar kuṭipōlum paṭippuk kālantoṭṭu mēm paṭṭu varuṅ kuṭi eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār parimēlaḻakar.
muttamiḻaracak kuṭikaḷuḷ pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyē mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyateṉṟum, piṉpu muṟiyē cōḻa cērak kuṭikaḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum oru vaḻimuṟic ceyti vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.

yirāmāyaṇattil cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar mūvarum kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar.

eṉṟum,
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ 	(poruḷ.381)
eṉṟum mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum, mutukuṭi eṉat tamiḻ maṟavar kulamum paḻimiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṟavar kuṭiyi,
kaṟṟōṉṟi maṇṭōṉṟāk kālattē vāḷōṭu 
 muṟṟōṉṟi mūtta kuṭi	(pu.po.ve.35)
eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyum.
pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa talikkaḻakam 
muttamiḻk kaḻakamāyiruntatiṉāl; ataṟkumuṉ muttamiḻ            tokutaṟkum, ataṟkumuṉ iyaṟṟamiḻ tōṉṟaṟkum, ataṟkumuṉ ilakkaṇamum ataṟkumuṉ ilakkiyamum ataṟkumuṉ eḻuttum ataṟkumuṉ moḻiyum tōṉṟaṟkum kaṇakkiṭa muṭiyāta kālañceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. poruḷatikārattil kūṟappaṭum aintiṇi niliyum taḻikoṇṭu cēṟal, iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutaliya pakutikaḷum, tamiḻar tuvakkantoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭiṟkē uriyar eṉpati uṇarttum. tamiḻ iyalpāyt tōṉṟiya moḻiyātalāṉum, teṟkiruntu vaṭakku nōkkiyē tirintu cellutalāṉum, mikat toṉmi vāyntatātalāṉum, atu tōṉṟiyatu kumarināṭē eṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭum.
paḻantamiḻar tam  muṉṉōrit  teṉpulattār eṉṟamiyālum,  teṟkē amiḻntupōṉa kumarināṭē tamiḻar tollakam eṉpatu ṇarappaṭum. etirt ticikaḷik kuṟikkum pōtu kiḻamēl teṉvaṭal eṉṟu kūṟuvatē marapu; mēlkiḻakku vaṭateṟku eṉṟu kūṟuvatu marapaṉṟu. nākarika makkaḷ kīḻiruntē mēṟcella vēṇṭiyiruppatālum, katiravaṉ kiḻakkē tōṉṟi mēṟkē maṟivatālum kiḻakku muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu. tamiḻar teṟkēyiruntē vaṭakku vantavarātaliṉ teṟku muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu eṉka.
5. tamiḻam eṉṉum peyarē tiraviṭam eṉat tirintatu
paṇṭik kālattil nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum moḻip peyarkaḷum perumpālum am īṟu peṟṟē vaḻaṅkiṉa. 
e-ṭu: ciṅkaḷam, kaṭāram, āriyam. tamiḻum tamiḻam eṉa vaḻaṅkik kulattiyum nāṭṭiyum moḻiyiyuṅ kuṟittatu. tamiḻak kūttu tamiḻa vaṇṇāṉ eṉṉum puṇarmoḻikaḷil, tamiḻ eṉṉuñcol akaraccāriyi peṟṟup puṇarntateṉṟu koḷvativiṭa, tamiḻam eṉṉuñ collē nilimoḻiyāka niṉṟu īṟukeṭṭup puṇarntateṉṟu koḷvatu cālac ciṟantatu. tamiḻam eṉpatu kulamum nāṭum moḻiyum ākiya mūṉṟiyum kuṟikkuñ collātaliṉ, teḷiviṉ poruṭṭu nāṭṭik kuṟikkumpōtu tamiḻakam eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar.
viyaka varippil tamiḻakaṅ kēṭpa (puṟam. 168:18).       tamiḻakam eṉṉum peyariyē paṇṭik kirēkka urōma carittirāciriyar `ṭamirikka (Damirica), `ṭamerikke (Damarice) eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkiṉar.
vaṭanāṭṭu āriya (camaskiruta) nūlkaḷil tiraviṭam eṉṉum col mutalāvatu tramiḷam eṉṟē vaḻaṅki vantatu. ḻakaram vaṭamoḻiyililli. cila uyirmeym mutalkaḷi rakarañ cērttu, tra pra eṉap puṇareḻuttukaḷākat tirippatu vaṭanūlār vaḻakkam. e-ṭu: paṭi - prati, pavaḷam - pravaḷam. itaṉāl, tamiḻam eṉṉuñ col tramiḷam eṉat  tirintatu iyalpē. piṉpu atu nāḷaṭivil tramiṭam traviṭam, eṉat tirintatu. ḷa - ṭa, ma - va, pōli. traviṭam eṉpatu meym mutalātaliṉ tamiḻil tiraviṭam eṉṟākip piṉpu tirāviṭam eṉa nīṇṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kumarināṭṭuc carittirattiyum tolkāppiyattiyum mēṟkaṇakku nūlkaḷiyum aṟiyāta varātaliṉ, tamiḻari vaṭakkiruntu vantavarākavum āriyarāl nākarikamaṭintavarākavum koṇṭu tramiḷam eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu tamiḻ eṉṉuñ col piṟantateṉṟu kūṟiṉār. āṉāl, paṇṭitar kiriyarcaṉ iti maṟuttut tamiḻam eṉpatē traviṭam eṉpataṉ mūlam eṉat tamatu intiya moḻiyārāycci (Linguistic Survey of India) eṉṉum nūlil nāṭṭiyuḷḷār. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
tamiḻukkut tiraviṭam eṉṉum peyar tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉum, tamiḻ eṉṉum vaṭivatti yoṭṭiya peyarkaḷē mēṉāṭṭilum vaṭanāṭṭilum paḻaṅkālattil vaḻaṅki vantamiyālum, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷellām oru kālattil tamiḻākavē yiruntamiyāṉum, tamiḻam eṉṉum peyarē tiraviṭam eṉat tirintatu eṉṟu teḷiyappaṭum.
6. tamiḻē piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷākat tirintami
tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar eṅṅaṉam tiraviṭam eṉṟu tirintatō, aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻākiya moḻiyum piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷāyt tirintateṉka.
talikkaḻakak kālattil, tamiḻ, centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉa iraṇṭāka vakukkappaṭṭatu. ivi vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷum centamiḻ nāṭu koṭuntamiḻ nāṭu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻnta paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷ koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷāyiruntaṉa.
ilakkaṇam nirampiya ciṟanta vaḻakkāṉa tamiḻ centamiḻ, atu tirintatu koṭuntamiḻ. cemmi nērmi; koṭumi vaḷivu. ceṅkōl koṭuṅkōl eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷi nōkkuka.
centamiḻc coṟkaḷ, iyaṟcol (Primitives) tiricol 
(Derivatives) eṉa iraṇṭāka vakukkappaṭṭaṉa. iyalpāṉa col iyaṟcol; atiṉiṉṟu tirikkappaṭṭa col tiricol. kiḷ, cēr eṉpaṉa iyaṟcoṟkaḷ; kiḷḷi, cēri eṉpaṉa tiricoṟkaḷ. iyaṟcol vēṟu; vērccol vēṟu; pakutiyum vēṟu. kiḷ eṉpataṟkuk kil eṉpatum, cēr eṉpataṟkuc cē eṉpatum vērc coṟkaḷām. vērccol muntiṉatu, iyaṟcol pintiṉatu. iṟṟi yiyaṟcoṟkaḷ tōṉṟāta mutaṟkālattil vērc coṟkaḷē iyaṟcoṟkaḷāyum, iyaṟcoṟkaḷē tiricoṟkaḷā yumiruntaṉa. vērccoṟkaḷ (1) āṇivērc coṟkaḷ (primary roots), (2) pakkavērc coṟkaḷ (secondary roots), (3) calli vērc coṟkaḷ (tertiary roots) eṉa mūvakippaṭum. vērc coṟkaṭkum mūlamāṉa vitic coṟkaḷ eṉpaṉa uṇṭu. aviyē cuṭṭu viḷakkattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa aiñcuṭṭukaḷ. pakuti eṉpatu vērc collāyum iyaṟcollāyum tiricollāyum ivaṟṟiṉ mikipāṭāyum paku colliṉ mutaṉiliyāyiruppatu. ivaṟṟiṟ keṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷi, muṟiyē, killiṉāṉ kiḷḷiṉāṉ kiḷittāṉ kiḷippaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum pakucoṟkaḷiṟ kāṇka. ivaṟṟiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷ centamiḻc colliyal neṟimuṟikaḷ eṉṉum nūlil viḷakkappaṭum. iṅṅaṉamellām coṉ mūlaṅkaḷi aṭinili varikkumē ārāyntu cellat tamiḻiltāṉ iyalum. āriya nūlkaḷil vērccoṟkaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭupavaṟṟiṟ pala tiri coṟkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷumē yeṉṟaṟika.
koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷ centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻap pala ticiyilum vaḻaṅkiṉamiyiṉ ticiccoṟka ḷeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟcol tiricol ticiccol eṉṉum mūṉṟuṭaṉ, iṭikkaḻakak kālattil putitāy vantu vaḻaṅkiya         orucila vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻiṟ kalanta ayaṉmoḻi yeṉṟa vakiyil vaṭacoṟka ḷeṉṟē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. akkālattil tamiḻiṟ kalanta ayaṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻi yoṉṟē. im muṟippaṭi ikkālattilum, tamiḻiṟ kalanta āṅkilac col pōrttukkīciyaccol mutaliyavaṟṟi avvam moḻip peyarāl āṅkilac col pōrttukkīciyaccol eṉak kūṟal vēṇṭumē yaṉṟit ticiccoṟkaḷeṉak kūṟutal kūṭātu. itupōtu kiḷimoḻikaḷāy allatu iṉamoḻikaḷāyp pirintupōyuḷḷa teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyaviyellām, paḻaṅkālattil koṭuttamiḻā yiruntatiṉālēyē koṭuntamiḻukku eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati maṟattal kūṭātu. koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷ allatu ticiccoṟkaḷ mūvakip paṭum. aviyāvaṉa:
(1)	poruḷ tirinta col.
	e-ṭu: ceppu (tamiḻ) = viṭicol;
	ceppu (teluṅku) = col.
(2)	vaṭivu tirinta col.
	e-ṭu: pōyiṉāṉ (ta.) - pōyiṉāṭu (te.)
(3)	putuccol.
	e-ṭu: aṭuku (te.) = kēḷ.
putuccollum maṟinta vērccol (ammu (te.) = Éš) maṟiyā vērccol (mŸ(fhJ)-mLF) eṉa iruvakippaṭum. iṅṅaṉam mūvakippaṭṭa ticiccoṟkaḷ mikunta allatu kalanta tamiḻ koṭuntamiḻām. tolkāppiyar,

eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl, nālvakic colluḷ vaṭacol ayaṟcol eṉpatum, ticiccol koṭuntamiḻ eṉpatum,  ēṉiyiraṇṭum centamiḻ eṉpatum, aṟiyak kiṭattal kāṇka.
centamiḻ nilam vikiyāṟṟiṉ mēṟkum marutayāṟṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkumām eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya piṟkāla uriyāciriyar urittaṉar. itil, vikiyāṟṟiṉ teṟkuḷḷa ciṟanta centamiḻ nilappakuti vilakkappaṭṭirup
pataṉālum, paḻaṅkālattil cēracōḻa pāṇṭiya muttamiḻ nāṭumē centamiḻ nilamāyiruntatiṉālum, iv vuri tavaṟākum.
centamiḻ nāṭāvatu: vikiyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum,
..... maruvūriṉ mēṟkum eṉpa. ivvāṟu urittaṟku ōr ilakkaṇaṅ kāṇāmiyāṉum, vikiyāṟṟiṉ teṟkākiya koṟkiyum karuvūriṉ mēṟkākiya koṭuṅkōḷūrum marutayāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkākiya kāñciyum tamiḻtiri nilamātal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, aḵtu uriyaṉṟu eṉpār urikkumāṟu:
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
 āyiṭit
 tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
 vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉa
 eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi
eṉṟamiyāṉum, itaṉuḷ tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakameṉa vicēṭittamiyāṉum, kiḻakkum mēṟkum elli kūṟātu teṟkelli kūṟiyavataṉāṟ kumariyiṉ teṟkākiya nāṭukaḷi yoḻittu, vēṅkaṭa maliyiṉ teṟkum kumariyiṉ vaṭakkum kuṇakaṭaliṉ mēṟkum kuṭakaṭaliṉ kiḻakkumākiya nilam centamiḻ nilameṉṟurippa eṉṟu teyvacciliyār piṟaṉuṭaṉpaṭṭatu tāṉuṭaṉpaṭutalākak kūṟiya uriyē uṇmiyāṉatām.
koṭuntamiḻ nāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭiyum,
teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭaṅ kuṭaṅkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
 paṉṟi yaruvā vataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāta
 cīta malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr 
 ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ
eṉṉum veṇpāvāṟ kuṟittu, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār ā erumi eṉpavaṟṟip peṟṟameṉṟum; kuṭanāṭṭār tāyit taḷḷiyeṉṟum, nāyi ñeḷḷi eṉṟum; kuṭṭanāṭṭār tantiyi accaṉ eṉṟum; kaṟkā nāṭṭār vañcarik kiyar eṉṟum; cīta nāṭṭār ēṭā veṉpataṉi eluvaṉ eṉṟum, tōḻiyi ikuḷi eṉṟum, tammāmi yeṉpataṉit tantuviyeṉṟum; pūḻi nāṭṭār nāyi ñamaliyeṉṟum, ciṟu kuḷattip pāḻiyeṉṟum, aruvā nāṭṭār ceyyic ceṟuveṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattik kēṇiyeṉṟum; aruvā vaṭa taliyār kuṟuṇiyik kuṭṭiyeṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa
eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliyōr kāṭṭuk kūṟiyatu 
ki.pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kēṟṟatātaliṉ, ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkēṟkātu.
iṉic ciṅkaḷam antō veṉpatu; karunaṭaṅkarik cikka kuḷira eṉpaṉa; vaṭuku ceppeṉpatu; tuḷu māmarattik kokkeṉpatu; oḻintavaṟṟiṟkum vantuḻik kāṇka eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum;
paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa:.... poṅkar nāṭu.... aruvā vaṭatali eṉpa. ivi centamiḻ (cērnta) nāṭeṉṟamiyāl piṟa nāṭātal vēṇṭumeṉpār utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭumāṟu:..... paṉṉirunila māvaṉa:
 kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teluṅkum kaliṅkamum eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, kūpakamum kollamum kaṭal koḷḷap        paṭutaliṉ, kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyik kollameṉak kuṭiyēṟiṉār pōlum, pañcattirāviṭameṉavum vaṭanāṭṭār urippavākalāṉ, avi yintum vēṅkaṭattiṉ teṟkātaluṅ kūṭāmiyuṇarka.
annilattu vaḻaṅkuñ collākic ceñcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattu varuvaṉa nīkkappaṭā, 
e- ṟu;
kuṭāvaṭi yuḷiyam eṉṟavaḻi kuṭāvaṭi eṉpatu kuṭakattār piḷḷikaṭku iṭṭapeyar. antō eṉpatu ciṅkaḷavar aiyō eṉpataṟkiṭṭa peyar. yāṉ taṟkariya varutu eṉṟavaḻi, karital eṉpatu karunāṭar viḷip poruḷuṇarak kūṟuvatu. ceppu eṉpatu vaṭukar collutaṟkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. kokku eṉpatu tuḷuvar māvukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. piṟavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa palavaṟṟiyum vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka eṉak koṇṭu kūṟṟākat teyvacciliyārum kūṟiyatē ēṟṟa uriyām.
tolkāppiyar kālattil koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷāyirunta paḻantīpamum ciṅkaḷamum karunaṭamum vaṭukum (teluṅkamum) kaliṅkamum piṟkālattil piṟamoḻi nāṭukaḷāy vēṟupaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. nāvalan tēyattiluḷḷa moḻi vēṟupaṭṭa nāṭukaḷ tamiḻuṭpaṭa mottam patiṉeṭṭākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ap patiṉeṭṭum piṟkālattil aimpattāṟāyp pirintu pōṉami purāṇaṅ kūṟum. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭil irunta allatu atiyaṭuttu irunta ilakkaṇavuriyāciriyar, akkāla nilik kēṟpa aṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ naṭuvoḻinta perumpākattit tokipaṟṟiya paḻiya marapi viṭāmal paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷākap pirittaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ cēra nāṭṭup pakutikaḷāṉa kuṭṭam kuṭam vēṇ malāṭu eṉpaṉa uḷḷaṉa, akkālattu maliyāḷamoḻi tōṉṟāmiyiṉ. naṉṉūlār taṅkālap paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷiyum tolkāppiyar kālap paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷiyum maṉattuṭkoṇṭu,
centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
 oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
 taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi
eṉa nūṟpāc ceytār. patiṉeṇ nilaṅkaḷi,
ciṅkaḷam cōṉakam cāvakam cīṉam tuḷukkuṭakam
 koṅkaṇam kaṉṉaṭam kollam teluṅkam kaliṅka 
 kaṅkam makatam kaṭāram kavuṭam kaṭuṅkucalam vaṅkam
 taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpa ṉēḻputivi tāmiviyē
eṉṟum ceyyuḷ kūṟum. itiṟ kūṟiya cāvakam cīṉam kaṭāram eṉpavi muṟkālak koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷic cērntaviyalla. tamiḻoḻinta patiṉēḻ nilaṅkaḷiṟ pala muṟkālak koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷā yiruntamiyiṉ koṭuntamiḻ nāṭallāta piṟavaṟṟiyuñ cērttuk kūṟiviṭṭār pavaṇantiyār.
centamiḻ tolkāppiyar kālattil vēṅkaṭam vari vaḻaṅkiyatālum, atiyaṭuttu vaṭakkuḷḷa kaṉṉaṭamun teluṅkum muṟkālattil koṭuntamiḻā yiruntamiyālum, avaṟṟukkappāl taṟpōtu āriya vaṭivāyuḷḷa makārāṭṭiram (marāṭṭi) kūrccaram (kucarātti) eṉpavaṟṟiyum tamiḻ teluṅku kaṉṉaṭattōṭu cērttup pañcat tirāviṭam eṉa vaṭanūlār paṇṭikkālattil vaḻaṅkiyamiyālum, vaṭanāṭṭāriya moḻikaḷil tiraviṭak kūṟāṉa coṟkaḷum neṟimuṟikaḷum kalantuḷḷamiyālum, mutali kūṭṭam mutaliya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ nēpāḷa nāṭṭu malivāṇar moḻiyilum vaḻaṅkuvatākat terikiṉṟamiyālum, intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkil peluccittāṉattiluḷḷa malivāṇar pirākuyi eṉṉum tiraviṭamoḻiyip pēcukiṉ ṟamiyālum, oru kālattil nāvalantēya muḻuvatum tamiḻallā viṭiṉum tiraviṭamāvatu vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ūkikkappaṭum.
iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭil ōraḷavu cevviyāyp         pēcappaṭum tamiḻ vaṭakku nōkkic ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tirintu  mutalāvatu koṭuntamiḻāyum piṉpu tiraviṭamāyum tirivatiyum, tiraviṭamum āriyakkalappāl kālāriyamāy māṟuvatiyum, ataṟkappāl muṟiyē ariyāriya moḻikaḷum muḻu āriya moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅkuvatiyum, āriyar paḻaṅkuṭic coṟkaḷi yellām akaṟṟiviṭṭu avaṟṟiṭattil āriyac coṟkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkuvatil kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntu ik kēṭṭuttoḻili vaṭakkiruntu teṉkōṭivari ceytu varuvatiyum nōkkiṉ,       mutaṟ kālattil oru moḻiyē nāvalantēya muḻutum teṉkōṭiyiliruntu vaṭakōṭi nōkkic ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tirintu vaḻaṅkiya teṉpatiyum, āriyak kalappillāviṭṭāl iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭil tiraviṭamē vaḻaṅkum eṉpatiyum uyttuṇaralām.
oru moḻiyē ciṟitu ciṟitāyp peyarntu vaḻaṅkiyami yālēyē, vaṭanāṭukaḷi vēṟṟumoḻi nāṭeṉṉātu moḻi peyar tēyam eṉṟaṉar muṉṉōr.
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum.	(kuṟun. 11)
tamiḻiṉiṉṟu mutalāvatu pirintupōṉa koṭun tamiḻmoḻikaḷ kūrccaramum makārāṭṭiramumāmum. ataṉ piṉ teluṅkum kaṉṉaṭamum maliyāḷamum muṟiyē pirintaṉa.
tamiḻ allatu tamiḻam eṉṉum peyar pōṉṟē, tiraviṭam eṉpatum, mutalāvatu centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ ākiya iruvakit tamiḻiyum orē moḻiyākakkoṇṭa avaṟṟi oruṅkē uṇartti vantatu. teluṅku taṉiyāyp pirintupōṉapiṉpu, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷi āntira tiraviṭa pāṣi eṉṉum iṇimoḻip peyarāl aḻittaṉar vaṭanūlār. ippeyaril tiraviṭam eṉṉum varumoḻi teluṅkoḻinta tiraviṭatti yellām oru moḻiyākak koṇṭatu. ip peyari 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kumarilapaṭṭar vaḻaṅkiṉār. piṉpu kaṉṉaṭamum maliyāḷamum pirintu pōṉapiṉ, tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar poruḷ curuṅkic cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut tamiḻp pakutiyi maṭṭum kuṟikkum ciṟappup peyarāyum, tiraviṭam eṉpatu orōviṭattut tamiḻiyum orōviṭattut tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyuṅ kuṟikkum peyarāyum vaḻaṅki vantaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār tiraviṭam eṉṉum collit tiraviṭamoḻikaḷiṉ potuppeyarākavum tokutip peyarākavum variyaṟuttaṉar.
cēranāṭṭut tamiḻ ki.pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari centamiḻāyum, piṉpu 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari koṭun tamiḻāyumiruntu, ataṉ piṉ āriyakkalappāl maliyāḷam         eṉṉum tiraviṭamoḻiyāyt tirintatu. itik kaṭikkaḻaka nūlkaḷālum, cilappatikāram, puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māli, cēramāṉperumāḷ ceyyuḷ, nampiyārūrar tēvāram, kulacēkarāḻvār perumāḷ tirumoḻi eṉṉum cēranāṭṭuc centamiḻ nūlkaḷālum, an nāṭṭuk kalveṭṭukaḷālum, ceppuppaṭṭayaṅkaḷālum, maliyāḷa nūlkaḷālum aṟiyalām.

eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, iv vaṭikaḷiṉ uriyil, vaṭaticikkaṇ  vaṭukoḻinta tiripuṭimoḻi palavuḷavāka lāṉ maliyelli kūṟi oḻinta tici mūṉṟiṟkum tiripiṉmiyāṟ kaṭalelli kūṟiṉāreṉiṉu mamiyum eṉṟu aṭiyārkku        nallāruṅ kūṟiyiruttalāl, tolkāppiyar kālattil kaṉṉaṭan tōṉṟavilli yeṉpatum, teluṅku koṭuntamiḻ niliyil niṉṟa teṉpatum,  vēṅkaṭa  vellikkut  teṟkiluḷḷa nilappakuti muḻutum  centamiḻē  vaḻaṅkiṉa  teṉpatum  aṟiyappaṭum. teluṅku pirintatu ki.pi. cumār 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟum, kaṉṉaṭam pirintatu ki.pi. cumār 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟum kūṟalām.

(1) tamiḻ allatu tiraviṭa makkaṭ perukkam
oru moḻiyār mikap palarākap perukiviṭiṉ, avar neṭuntoli ceṉṟu parava nērum. appōtu taṭpa veppanili vēṟupāṭṭālum cuṟṟuc cārpiṉālum paḻañcol        māṟṟiṉālum taṉi viruppattālum coṟkaḷ tiriyavum putitākat                 tōṉṟavum peṟum. potuvāka, cumār 500 kal tolivāyiṉ putuc coṟkaḷ tōṉṟuvatum moḻi tirivatum iyalpu.
(2) eḻuttolit tiripu
itu taṭpaveppanili vēṟupāṭṭālum cōmpalālum nikaḻvatu .
maliyāḷam	kaṉṉaṭam	teluṅku
na - ña	pa - ha	ḻa - ṭa - taṭpaveppanili
 ai - a	ai - e	ṇa - ṉa - cōmpal
iṉi ūṇ, toḻil, paḻakkavaḻakkam, ayaṉmoḻik kalappu mutaliyavaṟṟālum eḻuttoli tiriyum. eḻuttolit
tiripu taṭpaveppanili ūṇ mutaliyavaṟṟāl tirivatu kiḷimoḻiyākkat (Dialectic variation)â‰F¡ kāraṇamām; cōmpalāl tirivatu olimuṟik kēṭām (Phonetic decay).
(3) col tiripu
(4) poruḷ tiripu
e-ṭu:	veḷḷam (ta.) = veḷḷiyāṉa putupperukku nīr; veḷḷam (ma.) = nīr.
(5) iyaṟkit terippu (Natural Selection)
tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻil vīṭṭik kuṟikkum oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷil, teluṅku il eṉṉuñ colliyum, kaṉṉaṭam maṉa eṉṉuñ colliyum terintukoṇṭaṉa.
(6) vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkal
v-L: J‹D = ij, (k.), tevu = teku(ka.). neyttōr = netturu (te.)
vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkalāvatu tamiḻil itupōtu pēccuvaḻakkaṟṟu eḻuttuvaḻakkil maṭṭumuḷḷa coṟkaḷ piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ pēccu vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkuvatu.
(7) kuṭiyēṟṟap pātukāppu (Colonial Preservation)
v-L: nghœ (k.)=nghœJ. kommu (te.) = kompu. kuṭiyēṟṟap  pātukāppāvatu   itupōtu  tamiḻil  vaḻaṅkā viṭiṉum  kumarināṭṭil  vaḻaṅkiyavākat  terikiṉṟa  coṟkaḷum col vaṭivaṅkaḷum  piṟa tiraviṭa  moḻikaḷiṟ  pātukākkap paṭṭiruttal.
(8) puttākkac col
e-ṭu: mēṭi(ma.) = vāṅku, veḷḷu (te.) = cel.
(9) tāyoṭu (tamiḻoṭu) toṭarpiṉmi.
(10) vaṭacoṟ kalappu.
(11) vaṭamoḻi eḻuttiyum ilakkaṇattiyum mēṟkoḷḷal.
(12) tamiḻiṉ paṇpaṭuttam (Cultivation).
8. tiraviṭattiṟku āriyak kalappāl vanta kēṭu.
 (1)	tāymoḻikkum cēymoḻikkum toṭarpu aṟavu.
 (2)	tiraviṭamoḻip piriviṉiyum makkaṭ piriviṉiyum.
 (3)	tiraviṭa volit tiripum coṟṟiripum.
  (4)	tiraviṭaccol vaḻakkoḻivum maṟivum.
  (5)	carittira maṟippum malivum.
  (6)	putuccollākkat taṭi.
  (7)	tiraviṭarkkut tāymoḻi yuṇarcciyiṉmi.
  (8)	tiraviṭak kali vaḷarcciyiṉmi.
  (9)	tiraviṭa moḻi maṟivu.
(10)	colvaḻi aiyappāṭu. (atāvatu cila coṟkaḷ āriyamā tiraviṭamā eṉṟu aiyuṟappaṭutal.)
āriyar vaṭamoḻiyit tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu collit          tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilellām eṇṇiṟantu vēṇṭāta vaṭacoṟkaḷip pukutti, tamiḻoḻintavaṟṟiṟku vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷiyum ilakkaṇattiyum vakuttuc carittiratti māṟṟi, tiraviṭa nākarikatti āriya nākarikamākak kāṭṭi, vaṭamoḻiyiyē tiraviṭat tāyeṉṟu karutumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭaṉar. tamiḻaral - tiraviṭarum vaṭamoḻit toṭarpi uyarvākavum tamiḻat toṭarpi iḻivākavum karuti, āriyac cārpillāta tamiḻiyum tamiḻariyum paḻikkiṉṟaṉar. itu, mūkkaṟiyaṉ piṟariyum mūkkaṟukkac coṉṉa katiyēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
9. tiraviṭa moḻikaḷellām cērntatē muḻuttiraviṭam
moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu vāyiṉāl. pala coṟkaḷ cērntē moḻi. coṟkaḷi ovvoṉṟāy vaḻaṅkātuviṭṭāl piṉpu moḻiyum vaḻakkaṟṟoḻiyum. eḻuttu vaṭiviliruppatu maṭṭum moḻivaḻakkaṉṟu. kōtiyam (Gothic), ilattīṉ, camaṟkirutam, mutaliya vaḻakkaṟṟa moḻikaḷellām eḻuttu vaṭivil iṉṉumuḷa. āriyac coṟkaḷit tēviyiṉṟik kalantatiṉāl avaṟṟiṉ niliyilirunta tiraviṭac coṟkaḷellām vaḻakkiṟantum maṟintum pōyviṭṭaṉa. ilakkiyamuḷḷa moḻiyāṉāl vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷ ilakkiyattiṟ pōṟṟappaṭṭirukkum. aḵtillātatāyiṉ mīṭpaṟa iṟantoḻiyum. varuṣam, vārtti, viyāti, vīraṉ, vētam, vittiyam mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ muṟiyē āṇṭu, col, nōy, maṟavaṉ allatu maḻavaṉ, maṟi, maruttuvam allatu paṇṭuvam mutaliya teṉcoṟkaḷi vaḻakku vīḻttiyuḷḷaṉa. ilakkiyamillāta vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭa moḻikaḷō, āriyac coṟ kalappāl tirintum maṟintum varukiṉṟaṉa. kōṇṭi (Gondi), patrī (Bhatri), mālṟṟō (Malto), pōy (Bhoi) mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ meḷḷa meḷḷa āriya mayamāvati allatu maṟintu pōvatip paṇṭitar kiriyarcaṉ 1906ām āṇṭē tamatu intiya moḻiyiyal aḷavīṭu eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār. tamiḻi āriyattiṉiṉṟu vilakkāviṭṭāl, ciṟitu ciṟitāyt teṟkē taḷḷippōy iṟutiyil teṉmoḻi teṉpulattār moḻiyākiviṭum.  ituvē āriyar virumpuvatu.
centamiḻum koṭuntamiḻum cērntatē tamiḻātalāṉum,         tamiḻal-tiraviṭa moḻikaḷellām paḻiya koṭuntamiḻkaḷē yātalāṉum, tiraviṭarellām oru kulattārēyātalāṉum, āriyak kalappiṉṟi avar pēcum coṟkaḷum iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷum tiraviṭamē yātalāṉum,  ellāt tiraviṭa moḻikaḷum cērntē muḻuttiraviṭamākum. tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ palavāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ, tamiḻ teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam maliyāḷam eṉṉum nāṉkumē ciṟantaṉavākum.
tiraviṭarkkuc ciṟappāka vuriya tūya-paḻiya-pala tuṟippaṭṭa-uyarnili ilakkiyam tamiḻilēyē yiruttalāṉum, tamiḻal-tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil uḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ piṟpaṭṭaṉavum palatuṟip paṭātaṉavum perumpālum āriyac cārpuḷḷaṉavum kaḻaka nūlnilikkut tāḻntaṉavumāyiruttalāṉum, tamiḻaral-tiraviṭarellām tattam ilakkiyattip pōṉṟē piṟa tiraviṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum pēṇuvatuṭaṉ tamiḻilakkiyattiyē ciṟappākap pēṇik kaṟṟaṟkuriyar. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkāta cila tūya, ciṟanta tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, tamiḻarum piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷip pēṇik kaṟṟaṟkuriyar. tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa tiraviṭac coṉṉūṟkali yaṉittum tiraviṭarellārkkum potuvuṭimiyeṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. tamiḻum piṟa tiraviṭamum muṟiyē ilakkiyattilum collilum oṉṟukkoṉṟu utavum nili tāyum makkaḷum pōla ātaliṉ, tamiḻaral-tirāviṭar iṉimēlāyiṉum āriyac cārpi iyaṉṟavari akaṟṟiviṭṭut tamiḻit taḻuvuvārāka.
āriyak kalappiṉālēyē māperun tiraviṭa nāṭu cīr kulintu ciṉṉapiṉṉamāyc citintu kiṭakkiṉṟateṉka. vittūṟṟi (Funnel) eṉṉum maliyāḷac collum, kempu eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac collum, eccarikki eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭa teluṅkuc collum tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavē.
tevukkoḷaṟ poruṭṭē.	(tol. 345)
ceyyuṉṉōṉ (ceynaṉ) eṉṉum maliyāḷac colliyum, teku (tevu) eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac colliyum, aṭṭa (aṭṭi) eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭa teluṅkuc colliyum1, aṟiyumpōtē, ac coṟkaṭkut tamiḻiṟ kūṟum poruḷ naṉṟāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu; vaḻuvun terikiṉṟatu.
1.	aṭṭiyāṭal eṉṉun toṭaril, aṭṭi yeṉpatu taliyillā muṇṭattik kuṟikkum. aṭṭa allatu aṭṭe eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭa teluṅkuc collukku ituvē poruḷ.
10. tamiḻp paṇpaṭuttam
cilar, vaṭanāṭṭil pirākirutam eṉṉum paḻiya moḻikaḷip paṇpaṭuttic camaṟkirutam amittāṟ pōlat teṉṉāṭṭilum tiraviṭamoḻikaḷip paṇpaṭutti amittatē centamiḻ eṉṟum, tamiḻukkup piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ kiḷimoḻi yeṉappaṭum cēymoḻika ḷākātu iṉamoḻi yeṉappaṭum uṭaṉpiṟappu moḻikaḷē yeṉṟum, tiraviṭac coṟkaḷiṉ vērkaḷiyellām centamiḻallāta tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiltāṉ kāṇavēṇṭum  eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivar tiraviṭa carittirattiyum moḻi nūliyum tamiḻiṉ iyalpiyum aṟintiruntāl iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟār.
 tamiḻ paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭatu uṇmiyē. āṉāl, appaṇpaṭuttam āriyamuṟiyil vērccol maṟiyumpaṭi tirittuc ceyyappaṭṭataṉṟu. mutaṟṟamiḻariṉ olimuṟiyum, coṟkaḷiṉ paḻiya allatu tirutta vaṭivam, nuṇporuḷ viḷakkamum, coṟṟoṭariṉ vaḻāniliyum kāttukkoḷḷum varampīṭē centamiḻap paṇpaṭuttamām.
ṭaṇa mutaliya (moḻimutalākā) veḻuttukaḷik koṇṭu mutaṟṟamiḻariṉ coṟkaḷ toṭaṅkavilli. ataṉāl, tamiḻukku mutaṉmutal ilakkaṇam vakuttapōtu, akkāli moḻimutalākā veḻuttukaḷ moḻimutaleḻuttukaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭila.
kocci vaḻakkil, coṟkaḷiṉ ṉakaravīṟukaḷ ṉ, m eṉṉum īreḻuttiṟ kiṭippaṭṭa oliyāyum, aikāra vīṟukaḷ akara ekara īṟukaḷākavum olikkum.
e - ṭu: nāṉ - nā(ṉ); vili - vila, naṭatti - naṭatte. ivi tamiḻil vaḻuvām; āṉāl, intiyilum piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷilum vaḻāniliyām. avarkaḷ eṉṉum colli avaka eṉṟu olippar. itu tamiḻil vaḻuvām. āṅkilattil vaḻā niliyām. (Pearl eṉṉum colliṉ rakatti nōkkuka). iṅṅaṉam tamiḻukku iyalpum vaḻāniliyum aṭippaṭiyāyiṉ, piṟamoḻikaṭkut tiripum vaḻāniliyum aṭippaṭiyām.
avaṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷ teluṅkil vāṭu, pōyiṉāṭu eṉat tirikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻil kāṉ eṉpatu kāṭu eṉat tirintu ciṟitu poruḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, vāṭu, pōyiṉāṭu eṉpaṉa aṅṅaṉamalla. varutu (varukiṟatu), pēyum (peyyum) mutaliya vaṭivukaḷum centamiḻkkuriyavalla.
mūri (mū - mūr - mūri) eṉpatu kiḻa erutu. iti maliyāḷat til erutu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭap potuppittal (Generalisation) nuṇporuḷ viḷakkattiṟku māṟāṉatu. oruvaṉ oruvaṉiṭattil oru poruḷik kēṭkumpōtu, iḻintōṉ ī eṉṟum, ottōṉ tā eṉṟum uyarntōṉ koṭu eṉṟum kūṟa vitittaṉar muṉṉōr. āṉāl, itit tamiḻarum taṟpōtu kikkoḷvatilli. nāṉ avaṉukkut tantēṉ. avaṉ eṉakkuk koṭuttāṉ eṉpaṉa viṉivaḻānilipaṟṟiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷ.
iṉi, puṇarccit tiripiyum paṇpaṭuttattiṉ pāṟpaṭut talām. mūvakip puṇarccit tiripukaḷuḷ, tirital oṉṟē paṇpaṭuttattiṉpāṟpaṭṭatu. atilum, tēṅkuṭam vēppili pōṉṟa tiripukaḷ ataṉpāṟpaṭā. kaṇ + kaṭi = kaṭkaṭi, 
maṇ + tāḻi = maṭṭāḻi, pal + poṭi = paṟpoṭi, kal + pu = kaṟpu, kal + tāḻi = kaṟṟāḻi, muḷ + ceṭi = muṭceṭi, naḷ + pu = naṭpu, tāḷ + tuṇi = tāṭṭuṇi, poṉ + kuṭam = poṟkuṭam, 
piṉ + pāṭu = piṟpāṭu, poṉ + taṭṭu = poṟṟaṭṭu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟaviyē paṇpaṭuttamākum. aṉpu + taḷi = aṟputtaḷi eṉpatu ceyyuṭkē yuriyatu. ēṉit tōṉṟal keṭutal ākiya iraṇṭum iyalpākavē nērntaṉa. tali + kaṭṭu = talikkaṭṭu eṉṟu tamiḻil puṇarntatu talakeṭṭu eṉṟu teluṅkil valikkātu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatēyeṉiṉ āriyattilum āriyat taṉmi cāṉṟa teluṅkilumuḷḷa valliṉaṅkaḷellām tamiḻ valliṉaṅkaḷiṉ iraṭṭiyātaliṉ, talakeṭṭu eṉṉum teluṅkup puṇarmoḻi ōciyil talikkaṭṭu eṉṉum tamiḻp puṇarmoḻiyi yottatē yeṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka.
tōṉṟal tiripu taṉittaṉi niṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ         iṇippik kāṭṭukiṟatu. colliṇippu poruḷiṇip         pik kuṟikkum. nilimoḻiyiṉ īṟṟuvali yiraṭṭalum iṭiniṉṟa meli valittalum iṅṅaṉamē. vāḻi, kāy, kāṭu, āṟu ; vēmpu,  ili eṉṉum coṟkaḷ taṉittaṉi niṉṟapōtu taṉittaṉip poruḷiyē uṇarttum. avi, vāḻikkāy kāṭṭāṟu vēppili eṉap puṇarnta pōtō, vāḻiyiṉ kāy, kāṭṭiluḷḷa āṟu, vēmpiṉ ili eṉa  avaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ toṭarpupaṭṭa niliyik kāṭṭum. mutaṉmutal im muṟiyik kiyāṇṭavar potumakkaḷē.
uyirum uyirum puṇarumpōtu yakaravakara uṭampaṭu meykaḷ tōṉṟuvatu emmoḻikkum iyalpām. āṉāl, ati varivaṭivil mutaṉ mutaṟ kāṭṭiṉavar tamiḻarē. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattil, 140ām nūṟpāvuriyil variyāreṉṟa taṉāṉ uṭampaṭumey kōṭal orutaliyaṉṟu. kiḷi aritu, mūṅkā illi eṉavum varum eṉṟum, 259ām nūṟpāvuriyil `atā aṉṟu eṉṉum eṭuttukkāṭṭum, 284ām nūṟpāvuri yil paṉāaṭṭu eṉṉum eṭuttukkāṭṭum, 294ām nūṟpāvuri yil kōoṉ eṉṉum eṭuttukkāṭṭum kūṟiyiruppatāl, tamiḻilum mutaṟkālattil uṭampaṭumeyyiṉṟi yeḻutap paṭṭateṉa ūkikkalām.
tiraviṭam tōṉṟiṉatu kumarināṭātalāṉum, kumarināṭṭu moḻi tamiḻēyātalāṉum, tiraviṭac coṟkaṭkellām perumpālum vēr tamiḻilēyē iruttalāṉum, tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iṉṉum tolli vaṭivilēyē vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil tirintuḷḷa orucila coṟkaḷum ulaka vaḻakkil iyalpu vaṭivil iruttalāṉum, tamiḻolikaḷellām iyalpum tūymiyumāyiruttalāṉum, tamiḻilēyē tiraviṭa vērpporuḷ kāṇa muṭiyum. itip piṟkāṭṭum eṭuttukkāṭṭu viḷakkaṅ kaḷāl paṭippōr tāmē veḷḷiṭimaliyāyk kāṇpar.
 11. kālṭuvel tamiḻip ciṟappittuk kūṟuvaṉa
tirāviṭa moḻiyum kiḷimoḻiyumāṉa ovvoṉṟiṉ ilakkaṇa amippiyum ataṉtaṉ takutikkum āciriyarkku       atiluḷḷa payiṟcikkum takkavāṟu mikavō kuṟiyavō ārāyntu viḷakkumpōtē, tām muppattēḻāṇṭukaṭku mēlākap payiṉṟu vantatum tam matattoṇṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiyatum tiraviṭa          moḻikaḷuṭ  perumpālum  mutaṉmutaṟ paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟatum taliciṟanta muṟiyil vaḷarkkap peṟṟatum palavakiyilum tiraviṭak kuṭumpattiṟkup patiṉmi tāṅkuvatumāṉa, tamiḻamippi aṭikkaṭi eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu āciriyariṉ ciṟappu nōkkamākum.
(tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇam - muṉṉuri, pa. 1)
ik kuṭumpam (tiraviṭam) oru kālattil airōppiya āciriyarāl tamiḻiyam (Tamulian or Tamulic) eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, tamiḻ perumpālum ik kuṭumpattil mikat toṉmiyāṉatum mika vuyarvākap paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟatum ik kuṭumpattiṟkuriya vaṭivaṅkaḷilum vērkaḷilum perumpālāṉa vaṟṟip peṟṟiruppatumāṉa moḻiyāyiruntālu.....
(.  - pa. 4)
tamiḻ-immoḻi tiraviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ perumpālum mutaṉmutal paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟatum mikunta vaḷamuḷḷatum aiyamaṟap paḻimiyāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷuḷ perumpakutiyiyum mikappala vakikaḷiyum koṇṭuḷḷatumāṉatātaliṉ, taṉ takutikkēṟṟapaṭi, paṭṭiyil mutalil kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
(.  - pa. 9)
evvakiyilum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷukkuḷ taliciṟappap paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟa tamiḻ vēṇṭumāyiṉ, vaṭamoḻit toṭarpi aṟavē vilakkuvatuṭaṉ. taṉittu vaḻaṅkutal maṭṭumaṉṟu; ataṉ utaviyiṉṟit taḻittōṅkavum iyalum.
ēṟattāḻa ellā nūlkaḷum eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa centamiḻ eṉṉum paḻiya allatu ilakkiyat tamiḻ mikamikak kuṟinta vaṭacoṟkaḷiyuṭiyatu; vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷiyum puṟakkaṇittut tūya paḻantiraviṭa             volikaḷiyum vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum vērkaḷiyumē koṇṭirup pataṟkuk kāraṇamāka  ūkkattōṭum  poṟāmiyōṭuṅ kūṭiya akkaṟi kāṭṭuvatil ulaka vaḻakku allatu urinaṭiyiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭuvatu. oru tamiḻc ceyyuḷ, piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ          pōlātu, tāṉ koṇṭuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ mikutikkut takkavāṟaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ kuṟivukkut takkavāṟē cuviyum           ilakkiyat taṉmiyum nirampiyateṉṟu karutappaṭum avvaḷavu,          iv vaṭacoṟ poṟāmi paṭitta tamiḻar maṉattil muṟṟilum patintuḷḷatu. mika votuṅkiyuḷḷa nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷil vāḻum naṉi tāḻntōr moḻi vaṭacoṟkaḷi vilakkun tiṟattil pēraḷavu ilakkiya moḻiyi ottirukkiṉṟatu.
(.  - pa. 49)
tamiḻ taṉṉiṭattuk koṇṭuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉavaṟṟi allatu aviyellāvaṟṟiyum uṭaṉē nīkkamuṭiyum. aṅṅaṉam nīkkuvataṉāl atu naṭi uyarntu mikat tūymiyum nērttiyumaṭikiṟatu.
(.  - pa. 50)
tamiḻiṉ ilakkiyap paṇpaṭuttattiṉ mikutoṉmi
piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiya naṭi avaṟṟiṉ ulaka naṭiyiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭiruppativiṭat tamiḻiṉ ilakkiya naṭi ataṉ ulaka naṭiyiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ii. tamiḻiṉ aḷaviṟanta colvaḷamum centamiḻilakkaṇa vaṭivukaḷiṉ tokivakikaḷum maṟṟoru cāṉṟākum. centamiḻilakkaṇam vaḻakkaṟinta vaṭivaṅkaḷum vilakkappaṭṭa vikutikaḷum viyappāṉa vaḻuvamitikaḷum ceṟintuḷḷaporuṭkaḷam.....jÄÊ‹ aḷaviṟanta colvaḷatti yāḻppāṇattu viṭiyūḻiyar (Missionaries) veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa oru paḷḷit tamiḻakarāti 58,500 coṟkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷamiyē kāṭṭiviṭum. iṉṉam avaṟṟōṭu, av vakarātiyi niṟivākka, pallāyiraṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum ticiccoṟkaḷiyum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa puṇarccoṟkaḷiyum cērkka vēṇṭiyatirukkum.

āṉāl, teluṅkiṟkuriya il, (te. illu) eṉṉuñ collum, kaṉṉaṭattiṟkuriya maṉi (ka. maṉa) eṉṉuñ collum vaṭa tamiḻiliruppatuṭaṉ camiyācamiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkavuñ ceykiṉṟaṉa. kuṭi eṉṉum iṉṉoru col tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum potuvāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ ilakkaṇamum coṟṟokutiyum iṅṅaṉam pēraḷavil tiraviṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷukkum vērkaḷukkum potuk kaḷañciyamākum.
iii. taṟkālak kaṉṉaṭamum taṟkālat teluṅkum 
tamiḻiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭuñ ceytikaḷil paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭamum, paḻa maliyāḷamum, tuḷuvum, tuṭavum, kōṇṭum, kūvum tamiḻuṭaṉ ottiruttal tamiḻiṉ toṉmikkum tūymikkum maṟṟoru cāṉṟākum.
iv. pala teluṅku vērkaḷum vikutikaḷum tamiḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ tiripāyuḷḷami, piṉṉavaṟṟiṉ miku toṉmiyi valimiyāy uṟutippaṭuttum.
v. tamiḻiṉ toṉmikku maṟṟoru cāṉṟu atiluḷḷa vaṭacol taṟpavaṅkaḷiṉ peruñcitivākum.
vi. tamiḻiṉ ilakkiyap paṇpaṭuttattiṉ mikutoṉmi kalveṭṭukaḷālum ūkittaṟiyappaṭum.
(.  - pa. 83-9)
(kiṟittava maṟiyil) aracarkaḷ (irājākkaḷ) nāḷākamam eṉṉum puttakaṅkaḷiṉ epirēya mūlattil, ki.mu. cumār 1000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ tarcīcu allatu oppīr eṉṉum tuṟimukattiṉiṉṟu cālōmōṉ kappalkaḷiṟ koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭa carakkukaḷik kuṟitta paṭṭiyil, mayilukkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa col ulakattil eḻuttiṟ kāṇappaṭum tirāviṭac coṟkaḷil muṟpaṭṭatākum. itu aracarkaḷil tuki eṉṟum nāḷākamattil tūki eṉṟum uḷḷatu.... mayilukkup paḻañ ceyyuḷiluḷḷa tūya tamiḻ maliyāḷap peyar (aḻakiya) tōkiyi yuṭiyatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tōki eṉpatu.
(.  - pa. 91)
tamiḻ ilakkiyam teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa vilakkiyattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu; maliyāḷa vilakkiyattiṟku mika muṟpaṭṭatu.
(.  - pa. 126)
kālṭuvel kaṇṭa moḻinūṉ muṭipukaḷ
moḻikaḷ moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uṟavip patiṟpeyarkaḷ (Pronouns) mika viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl, patiṟpeyarkaḷ, ciṟappākat taṉmi muṉṉili yorumip peyarkaḷ, piṟa colvakikaḷiviṭa mikutiyāka nilipput taṉmiyik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; pallūḻik kaṭappilum mikac ciṟitē tirivaṉavākap potuvākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉum, vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷiṉum, viṉi vikutikaḷiṉuṅ kūṭa mika nilittaviyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa; avi piṟavaṟṟip pōṉṟē tirivataṟkiṭamiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ toṭarpukaḷum kiḷippukaḷum, kālattālum iṭattālum ettuṇi akaṉṟu kiṭappiṉum ēṟattāḻa makkaḷmoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭum. cilaviṭattu, mūviṭap peyarkaḷ maṭṭumē.
mutalāvatu orē kuṭumpattic cērn taṉavāyiruntu piṉpu kālak kaṭappiṉālum tiripiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉālum potu muṟiyāka vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷa moḻikaḷiṉ toṭarpuk kuṟippāy allatu uṟavuc cāṉṟāyuḷḷaṉa. ik kuṟippu, ciṟappākac colvakikaḷ yāvaṟṟilum mika nilippāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟa taṉmip peyarkaḷit taḻuvum.
(.  - pa. 254)
piṉvarum colvaricikaḷ nām ilakkaṇa oppīṭṭiṉāl kaṇṭa muṭipukkē tattam aḷavil taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. atāvatu : tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ, intiya airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattiṉ mututāyākak karutappaṭukiṉṟa - vaṭamoḻikku muṟpaṭṭa - āriya moḻiyoṭu tamakkuḷḷa toṉmiyāṉa vērūṉṟiya toṭarpik kāṭṭumpōtē, avaṟṟukkuc cittiya (Scythian) moḻik kuṭumpattuṭaṉ, ciṟappāka ukkirīya (Ugrian) moḻikaḷuṭaṉ, uḷḷa uṟavaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ mottattil mika neruṅkiyum mikat teḷivāyum mikamukkiyamāyum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē.
(.  - pa. 452)
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu tiraviṭa moḻikaḷi, ciṟappākat tamiḻi, ārāyntu pala uṇmikaḷik kaṇṭāreṉiṉum, tolkāppiyattiyum, kaḻaka (caṅka) nūlkaḷiyuṅ kallāmiyālum, kumarināṭṭu varalāṟṟi aṟiyāmiyālum, tamiḻiṉ tiraviṭat tāymiyik kāṇātatuṭaṉ, tiraviṭa nākarikatti āriya nākarikamākap piṟaḻak koṇṭu cila taṉit tamiḻc coṟkaḷiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar. āyiṉum, kaḻakanūṟ payiṟciyum taṉit tamiḻuṇarcciyum illāta akkālattil ayalnāṭṭārāṉa avar āriyattiṉiṉṟu tiraviṭattip pirittuk kāṭṭiṉa attuṇiyē mikac ciṟantateṉṟu koṇṭu perumakiḻcciyuṭaṉ avarukku eṉṟum naṉṟi pārāṭṭak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷōm.
tamiḻil - tiraviṭac coṟ pākupāṭu
tamiḻallāta piṟa tiraviṭac coṟkaḷellām, 1.brªjÄœ¢ coṟkaḷ, 2 koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷ, 3.tlbrh‰fŸ eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
1
maliyāḷam
maliyāḷap peyar
kēraḷam, maliyāḷam eṉpaṉa taṟpōtu maliyāḷa nāṭṭiyum, aṅku vaḻaṅkum moḻiyiyum kuṟikkum peyarkaḷām.
kaḻaka (caṅka)k kālattil muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷil oṉṟāyirunta cēranāṭṭiṉ mēlpākamē ippōtu maliyāḷa nāṭā yirukkiṉṟatu. kīḻppākam koṅkunāṭum (kōyamputtūr, cēlam , kōṭṭakap pakutikaḷ), kaṅkanāṭum (cēlam micūrc cīmip pakutikaḷ) ākum. maṟaṉ, tiṟaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ muṟiyē maṟal, tiṟal eṉṟu tirintāṟ pōla, cēraṉ eṉṉum peyarum cēral eṉap pōliyākip piṉpu aṉ īṟu peṟṟuc cēralaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
e-ṭu: cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ; kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nār muṭic cēral, takaṭūr eṟinta peruñcēral, kuṭakkō iḷañ cēral.
teṉṉavaṉ cēralaṉ cōḻaṉ		 (tiruvācakam)
ceṟumā vukikkum cēralaṉ kāṇka 	 (tirumukappācuram)
cēralaṉ eṉṉum peyar muṟiyē kēralaṉ, kēraḷaṉ eṉa maruviṟṟu,  im marūu vaṭivaṅkaḷ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ōriṭattuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli; pintiya nūlkaḷiltām kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl ivi piṟkālattaṉa eṉpatu teḷivu.
ca-ka, pōlio.nō: cīrtti-kīrtti, cempu-kempu (ka.) 
la-ḷa, pōli, o. nō: cetil - cetiḷ, vēli - vēḷi.
pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar pāṇṭiyam eṉa īṟu tirintu pāṇṭiyaṉ ceytikaḷik kuṟittāṟpōla, kēralaṉ kēraḷaṉ  eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, kēralam kēraḷam eṉa īṟu tirintu cēra nāṭṭiyum, an nāṭṭu moḻiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭelli 
mēṟkut toṭarcci (kuṭa) malikku mēṟkē, vaṭakkil maṅkaḷūri(maṅkaḷapuram)liruntu teṟkil tiruvaṉantapuram varikkum, teṉkaṉṉaṭam malapār kocci tiruvitaṅkōṭu (tiruvāṅkūr) eṉṉum nāṉku cīmikaḷil tāymoḻiyākap pēcappaṭuvatu maliyāḷam.
teṉṉi marattiṉ vaṭamoḻip peyarākiya nāḷikēram eṉpatu, kēram eṉa mutaṟkuṟiyāyp piṉpu kēraḷam eṉa virintu am maram mikutiyāy vaḷarum maliyāḷa nāṭṭik kuṟittateṉpatu poruntak kūṟum poymmik kūṟṟākum. vaṭamoḻikkum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkum uyarvum ataṉāl tamiḻukkum tamiḻarkkum iḻivum kaṟpikkappaṭṭa purāṇak kālattil, vaṭamoḻit toṭarpi uyarveṉa mayaṅkiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭār, kēraḷam eṉṉum peyarkku vaṭamoḻi mūlattiyum, kēraḷa nāṭṭiṟkup paracurāmakṣēttiram eṉṉum putuppeyariyum makiḻcciyāy ēṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. paracurāmarkku muntiyē vaḻaṅkiya peyar cēranāṭu eṉpatum, avar iṟutik kālattil tavañ ceyta iṭamākak kūṟiyiruppatu cēranāṭṭiṟku vaṭakkiluḷḷa kaṭaṟkarippakuti yeṉpatum aṟital vēṇṭum. *
patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil oru nampūtirip pārp paṉarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟa kēraḷōtpatti eṉṉum purāṇattil, pirāmaṇar pañcāpiṉiṉṟu paracurāmarāl teṉkaṉṉaṭattiluḷḷa kōkarṇattil mutal mutal kuṭiyēṟṟap paṭṭaṉar eṉṉum ceyti kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iti S. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār tamatu tamiḻārāycci (Tamil Studies) eṉṉum nūlil (pa. 348) maṟuttu, pirāmaṇarik kōkarṇattil kuṭiyēṟṟiyavaṉ kaṭampa marapiṉariṉ mutalvaṉāṉa mayūravarmaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ kālam ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyeṉṟuṅ kūṟi yuḷḷār. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
*	paṇṭic cēranāṭu kūrccaram (kujarat variyil toṭarntiruntatālum, vaṭanāṭṭu āriyar teṉṉāṭṭilum vantu kuṭi pukuntatālum, paracurāmar tavañceyta iṭattiṟku ēṟpaṭṭa paracurāma kṣēttiram eṉṉum peyar avviṭattōṭu toṭarnta teṉpākattiyum piṟkālattil taḻuvalāyiṟṟu. intu (cintu) eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭup pakutiyiṉ peyar teṉṉāṭṭiyum taḻuviṉāṟpōla.
maliyāḷam eṉṉum peyar aṇmiyil tōṉṟiya tākum. cēranāṭu malināṭātalāl cēranāṭṭāṉ maliyāḷi yeṉappaṭṭāṉ. mali+āḷi=maliyāḷi. āḷi=āḷ. mutalāḷi, toḻilāḷi, vaṅkāḷi, paṅkāḷi mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka. maliyāḷiyiṉ nāṭum moḻiyum maliyāḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
o. nō : vaṅkāḷi - vaṅkāḷam.
maliyāḷat tiripu
cēranāṭu ki.pi. 10M« ü‰wh©L tiu brªjÄHurU« brªjÄœ¥ òytU« âfœªj brªjÄœ ehlhÆUªjJ.* aiṅkuṟu nūṟum patiṟṟuppattum ākiya kaḻaka nūlkaḷum, cilappatikāram ākiya kaḻaka maruviya nūlum, puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉāriṉ ātiyulāvum, kulacēkara āḻvāriṉ perumāḷ tirumoḻiyum maliyāḷa nāṭeṉappaṭum cēra nāṭṭil eḻunta centamiḻ nūlkaḷē.
patiṉālām nūṟṟāṇṭil (ki.pi. 1320)vīrarākava cakkaravarttiyāl poṟikkappaṭṭa kōṭṭayam ceppēṭṭu moḻi vātil (vāyil) oṇṭāyil (uṇṭākil), eḻunnaḷḷi (eḻuntaruḷi) mutaliya cila maliyāḷat tiripukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya tamiḻē. atē nūṟṟāṇṭil (ki.pi. 1350) iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kaṇṇacap paṇikkar irāmāyaṇa moḻiyum ittakiyatē.
e-ṭu: koṇṭalin nēriruṇṭu curuṇṭu nīṇṭoḷi vārntu tiṅṅum, kuntaḷa pāramōṭu mukil kulattiṭa miṉṉal pōlē, puṇṭarīkēkṣaṇan narikep polintavaḷa cīta connāḷ.
itil, kuntaḷapāram  puṇṭarīkēkṣaṇaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭē  vaṭacoṟkaḷ.  ivaṟṟuḷḷum  kuntaḷam  eṉpatu  
*	naṉṉūṟ ciṟappuppāyirattil kuṇakaṭal kuṭakam kumari vēṅkaṭam, eṉunāṉ kelliyi ṉiruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttalāl 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil cēranāṭu koṭuttamiḻ nāṭāyiruntami peṟappaṭum.
kūntal - eṉṉum teṉcol tiripu: vārntu - vārnnu tiṇuṅkum - tiṅṅum. iṭi - iṭa. arukē - arike.
pōrttukkīciya viṭittoṇṭar (Missionaries) mutaṉmutal maliyāḷak kariyil vantiṟaṅkiṉar eṉṟum, maliyāḷa nāṭṭu moḻiyit tamakku muntiya arapiyar pōla malapār eṉṟaḻittaṉar eṉṟum, kīḻkariyilum ilaṅkik kariyilum vaḻaṅkiya moḻi (tamiḻ) ati yottirukkak kaṇṭu atiyum ap peyarāl aḻittaṉar eṉṟum avarkaḷ maliyāḷak kariyiluḷḷa ampalakkāṭṭil 1577 allatu 9il mutaṉ mutal tamiḻeḻuttil acciṭṭa puttaka moḻiyi malavār allatu tamiḻ (Malavar or Tamil) eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar eṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār kūṟukiṉṟār.   (muṉṉuri pak. 10-12)
itaṉāl, 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari maliyāḷa nāṭṭu moḻi koṭuntamiḻāyirunta teṉṟum, ataṉ piṉṉarē vaṭamoḻik kalappāl maliyāḷamākat tiriyat toṭaṅkiṟ ṟeṉṟum aṟiyalām. maliyāḷam mikat teḷivāyp pirintupōṉatu 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil (ki.pi. 1650) āriya veḻutti vakuttum, peruvāriyāy āriyac coṟkaḷiyum vikutikaḷiyum pukuttiyum, cēranāṭṭu moḻiyic cititta tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉālēyē.
1860 ām āṇṭiltāṉ mutal maliyāḷa ilakkaṇamum eḻuntatu. 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari maliyāḷiyar tamiḻiyum kaṟṟuvantaṉareṉṟum ataṉ piṉputāṉ av vaḻakkam aṭiyōṭu viṭappaṭṭateṉṟum mutiyōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭil pārppaṉaroḻinta maṟṟak kulattā rellām tamiḻar allatu tiraviṭarē. kuyavar, paṇikkar, pāṇar,  āyar, īḻavar, kavuṭar (kavuṇṭar) mutaliya pala kulattiṉar tamiḻnāṭṭilum uḷḷaṉar. nāyar, nāyāṭi, vāriyar, ceṟumaṉ mutaliya kulattiṉar maliyāḷattiṟkuc ciṟappāyirunta tālum, avar kuṭippeyarellām taṉit tamiḻē. tāḻntatu uyarntatu eṉṟu muṟiyē poruḷ tarum kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṉum ticippeyarkaḷ kuṭamalik kīḻnāṭṭiṟkē ēṟṟavi. ivi maliyāḷattilum vaḻaṅkuvatu maliyāḷanāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭup pakutiyē yeṉpatiyum maliyāḷiyar tamiḻ marapiṉarē eṉpatiyum uṇarttum. piṟkālattil  mēṟkut ticikkup paṭuñāyiṟu eṉa oru peyari avarkaḷ puṉintu koṇ ṭālum, atuvum taṉittamiḻē eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭu allatu cēranāṭṭut tuṟimukaṅkaḷ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil peruṅ kaṭal vāṇika niliyaṅkaḷākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

maliyāḷattiluḷḷa paḻimiyāṉa ūrp peyar kaḷellām iṉṟum taṉittamiḻāyē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭu (kōḻikkōṭu - Calicut), cēri (taliccēri), kuḷam (erṇāk kuḷam), puram (aṅkāṭipuram), nāṭu (valiyanāṭu), ūr (kaṇṇaṉūr), kuṉṟam (pūṅkuṉṉam), kā (kaṉṉaṅkāvu), kāṭu (pālikkāṭu - Palghat), kuṭi (cākkuṭi), vācal (paḷḷivācal), aṅkāṭi (parappaṉaṅkāṭi), tōṭṭam, pāṭi, tuṟi (tiruppuṉittuṟi), kuṟicci, ēri, kari (koṭṭārak kari), kaḷam (tiruvañcik kaḷam), iruppu mutaliyaṉa taṉittamiḻ ūrp peyarīṟukaḷām. kōṭu = mali. kā=cōli.
paḻañcēranāṭṭiṉ kīḻppakutiyiṉ teṉpākam (koṅku nāṭu)  iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭā yirukkiṉṟatu; vaṭapākattiṟ kaṉṉaṭam pukuntuḷḷatu.
12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa vēṇāṭu, pūḻi nāṭu, kuṭṭa nāṭu, kuṭa nāṭu, mali nāṭu eṉpaṉa iṟṟi maliyāḷa nāṭṭup pakutikaḷāyuḷḷaṉa.
pūḻiyaṉ utiyaṉ koṅkaṉ poṟiyaṉ
 vāṉavaṉ kaṭṭuvaṉ vāṉa varampaṉ
 villavaṉ kuṭanāṭaṉ vañci vēntaṉ
 kollic cilampaṉ kōti kēraḷaṉ
 pōntiṉ kaṇṇikkōṉ porunit tuṟivaṉ
 cēraṉ maliyamāṉ kōc cēraṉ peyarē 
eṉpatu tivākaram.
ivaṟṟuḷ koṅkaṉ, kollic cilampaṉ, poruni (āṉporuni)t tuṟivaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, kōyamputtūr cēlam ākiya iru kōṭṭakaṅkaḷum cēranāṭṭic cērntaṉa eṉpati viḷakkum. koṅkaṉ = koṅku nāṭṭaracaṉ. koṅku nāṭu kōyamputtūrk kōṭṭakap pakuti. kollimali cēlaṅ kōṭṭakattic cērntatu. cērar kuṭiyil oru kiḷiyiṉarāṉa atikamāṉ marapiṉar cēlattic cērnta takaṭūri (tarmapuriyi)t talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭaṉar.
kuṟṟiyalukarattip puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭal, titiyip pakka meṉṟal mutaliya paḻantamiḻ vaḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟum maliyāḷa nāṭṭil tāṉuḷḷaṉa.
toṉṟutoṭṭuc cēranāṭṭu melittal tiripu  coṟkaḷ cila cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷilum iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: tirinavum (tol.83) paḻaṉi, (puṟam 113) paḻuṉiya (maṇi,328) aṟiyuṉaṉ (puṟam 134) (tirikiṉṟa tirikuṉṉa - tiriyuṉṉa - tiriyuṉa-tiriṉa-tirina) paḻuttu - paḻuṉṉu - paḻuṉṉu - paḻuni - paḻuti, makiḻkiṉṟāṉ - makiḻuṉṉāṉ - makiḻunaṉ - makiḻnaṉ. vāḻkiṉṟāṉ - vāḻuṉṉāṉ - vāḻunaṉ - vāḻnaṉ - vāṇaṉ. iṉi, tiriyum - tiriyuṉ + a = tiriyuṉa - tiriṉa - tirina eṉṟumām. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum, ṉakarattiṉum nakaram muntiṉateṉak koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu. āṅkaṉam - aṅkaṉam - aṅṅaṉam - aṉṉaṇam.
āō vākum peyarumā ruḷavē.
 āyiṭaṉ aṟital ceyyu ḷuḷḷē	(tol. 680)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatum cēranāṭṭiṟkē ciṟappāy ēṟkum.

aṟiyalām.

pōraṭu tāṉip polantārk kuṭṭuva	(patit. 43)
eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattilum pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷa cēra nāṭṭuc cīriya ma ṟam iṉṟu āriya aṭimippaṭṭu aṇuvaḷavut tamiḻuṇarcci yiṉṟik kiṭappatu niṉikkuntōṟum neñcip puṇ paṭuttuvatā yirukkiṉṟatu.
maliyāḷam tirintataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1)	cēranāṭu  perumpālum  malittoṭarāl  taṭukkap paṭṭup piṟa tamiḻ nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ perun  toṭarpu koḷḷātiruntami.
(2)	12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭōṭu pāṇṭiya marapum 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭōṭu cōḻa marapum cēra marapuṭaṉ maṇavuṟavu niṟuttiyami.
(3)	vaṭamoḻikkum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkum teyva 
uyarvu kaṟpikkappaṭṭamiyum varampiṟanta vaṭacoṟ 
kalappum.
(4)	miku maḻiyāl maliyāḷiyarkku mūkkoli ciṟantami.
(5)	maliyāḷiyar muṉṉōriṉ centamiḻ nūlkaḷik kallāmi.
(6)	maliyāḷiyariṉ olimuṟic cōmpal.
maliyāḷam tirinta muṟikaḷ
(1)	muṟṟuviṉikaḷ pāl kāṭṭum īṟiḻattal
	e-ṭu: vantāṉ - vantu.
(2)	melittal tirivu
	e-ṭu:	eḻuntu-eḻunnu, aṅku-aṅṅu, kaḻuku - kaḻuṅṅu, kuñci-kuññi, vīḻntu-vīṇu.
(3)	nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉ kiṉṟatu eṉṉum iṭinili uṉṉu eṉat tirital
	e-ṭu:	ceykiṉṟa - ceykuṉṉu - ceyyuṉṉu.
(4)	vēṟṟumi yuruput tirivu.
	e-ṭu:	atiṉukku - (atiṉku) - atiṉṉu, uṭiya - uṭē- ṭē - ṟē.
(5)	pōlit tirivu
	e-ṭu:	narampu - ñarampu, ceymmiṉ - ceyviṉ.
  (6)	koccit tirivum tokuttalum
	e-ṭu: uḷḷa - oḷḷa, mali - mala, akaṟṟuka-akattuka, iru-iri, puṟā - prāvu, kaṉā - kiṉāvu, vēṇṭum - vēṇam, ceyyavēṇṭum - ceyyēṇṭu, pōka vēṇṭum - pōkēṇam.
  (7)	ṟakara rakara vēṟupāṭiṉmi
	e-ṭu:	uṟavu - uravu.
  (8)	nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattiṟkup patilāka vāṉ pāṉ īṟṟu viṉiyeccaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷum vaḻaṅkal.
	e-ṭu:	kuṭippāṉ = kuṭikka, naṭappāṉ - naṭakkāṉ = naṭakka, varuvāṉ = vara.
  (9)	ciṟappuc coṟkaḷ potup poruḷil vaḻaṅkal
	e-ṭu:	veḷḷam (putup perukku nīr), = nīr. mūri  (kiḻerutu) =  erutu, nōkku (kūrntu pār) = pār.
(10)	coṟkaḷi orumaruṅku paṟṟiya poruḷil vaḻaṅkal
	e-ṭu:	atē = ām, vaḷarē = mika, mati = pōtum; 
valiya = periya.
(11)	oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkāta oṉṟi vaḻaṅkal
	e-ṭu:	col (ta.) - giw (k.); kūppiṭu (ta.) - viḷi (ma.)
(12)	āy eṉṉum viṉiyecca vīṟu āyiṭṭu eṉa 
vaḻaṅkal
	e-ṭu:	civappāy - civappāyiṭṭu.
(13)	 neṭuñcuṭṭu vaḻakku vīḻāmi
	e-ṭu:	āyāḷ (av āḷ), ī mūri (im mūri)
(14)	ēval viṉikaḷ perumpālum viyaṅkōḷ vaṭivil vaḻaṅkal.
	e-ṭu: tā-tarika, tara.
(15)	tuṇi viṉi vēṟupāṭu
	e-ṭu: paṟiyuvāṉ kaḻiyum (colla muṭiyum), paṟiyuvāṉ kaḻiyuṉṉilla (collamuṭiyātu).
(16)	ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu mūviṭattum vaḻaṅkal.
	e-ṭu: ñāṉ, nī, avaṉ - pōkum.
(17)	ṟaṉṉakaram tannakaramāka eḻutappaṭal
(18)	vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkal
	e-ṭu: kinīṭṭak kācu, tampirāṭṭi.
(19)	putuc coṟkaḷ
	e-ṭu: mēṭi = vāṅku,  veṭippi = tuppuravākku.
 (20)	viṉimarapu vēṟupāṭu
	v-L: K£l òG§¼f (mÉ¡f), És¡F bfL¡f (miz¡f), K£L¡ F¤Jf (bfhL¡f), fhYw K¿¡f (bt£Lf.), maṭaṅṅi ( tirumpi) varuka.
kuṟippu :	cēranāṭṭuk koṭuntamiḻākiya maliyāḷattiluḷḷa iruvakit tamiḻccoṟkaḷiyum vaḻakku vīḻttaṟkum maliyāḷattār tamiḻc coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉkoṇṭu ataṉāl avarkkum tamiḻarkkum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭātapaṭiyum, āriyar vaṭacoṟkaḷi mikutiyāyk kalantu maliyāḷattik keṭuttut tamiḻukku mika ayaṉmippaṭuttiviṭṭaṉar.
maliyāḷac col varicikaḷ
(i) peyarc coṟkaḷ
1. mūviṭappeyar
	taṉmi	muṉṉili	paṭarkki	taṟcuṭṭu
 orumi:	ñāṉ	nī	avaṉ, ivaṉ	tāṉ
 			avaḷ, ivaḷ
 			atu, itu
paṉmi:	nām	niṅkaḷ	avar, ivar	taṅṅaḷ
 	nammaḷ		ava, iva	tāṅṅaḷ
 	ñaṅkaḷ			tāṅkaḷ
2. viṉāp peyar
	āṇ	peṇ	potu	aḵṟiṇi
orumi:	evaṉ	evaḷ	ār	etu, yātu, entu
paṉmi:		evar,	yār	eva, yāva
 		yāvar
eṉṉatu (ta.) = entu (ma.)
3. muṟip peyar
appaṉ, amma, ammāyi (pāṭṭi), makaṉ, makaḷ, marumakaṉ, marumakaḷ, pēraṉ mutaliya muṟippeyarkaḷ centamiḻc coṟkaḷākum.
mūttaccaṉ, mūttappaṉ, pērappaṉ; mūttamma, mūttacci; valiyaccaṉ (periyappaṉ), valiyappaṉ, iḷiyappaṉ kuññaccaṉ (ciṟṟappaṉ), peṇṇappaṉ (māmaṉ), peṇṇamma (māmi), keṭṭiyavaṉ (kaṇavaṉ), keṭṭiyavaḷ (maṉivi) mutaliya muṟippeyarkaḷ koṭun tamiḻc coṟkaḷākum.
jyēṣṭaṉ, jyēṣṭatti, aṉujaṉ, aṉujatti mutaliya vaṭacoṟ peyarkaḷ vaḻakkūṉṟiyatāl, aṇṇaṉ, akki, tampi, taṅki mutaliya centamiḻp peyarkaḷ maliyāḷattil vaḻakku vīḻntaṉa.
acci, appacci eṉṉum orupāṉ muṟippeyarkaḷ centamiḻ nāṭṭil cila kulattāriṭi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iviyum ivaṟṟiṉ āṇpāl vaṭivaṅkaḷāṉa accaṉ, appaccaṉ eṉpaṉavum oruṅkē maliyāḷa nāṭṭiltāṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kuṟippu: attaṉ-accaṉ, atti-acci, ta-ca, pōli.
4. makkaṭ peyar
aṭiyār, appakkāraṉ, appaṭṭaṉ, āḷ, āḷ kolli, iṭayaṉ, irappāḷi, iḷiyavaṉ, uṭayavaṉ, ūmaṉ. eḷiyavaṉ, kaṇiyāṉ, kaḷḷaṉ, kaṉṉiyāḷ, kuṭṭi (piḷḷi), kuṭiyaṉ,           kuruṭaṉ, kūlikkāraṉ, kollaṉ, cakkāḷ (cekkāṉ), ceviṭaṉ, ceṟiyavaṉ (ciṟiyavaṉ), taccaṉ, tāntōṉṉi (tāṉtōṉṟi),           tūtaṉ, tōḻaṉ, nallaṉ, pāṇaṉ, pital (piyaṉ), pōkkiri, maṭiyaṉ, mantiri, malayāḷi, mutalāḷi, mūppaṉ, vaṇṭikkāraṉ, vampaṉ, valiyavaṉ, vaḻippōkkaṉ mutaliyaṉa.
paṟayuṉṉavaṉ ceyyuṉṉavaṉ mutaliya viṉiyā
laṇiyum peyarkaḷum, kāṇi tiṉṉi mutaliya ceyvāṉ peyarkaḷun tamiḻmuṟiyi lamintaviyē.
5. vilaṅkup peyar
āṉa (āṉi), āṭu, eruma (erumi), oṭṭakam, kaḻuta (kaḻuti), veruku, karaṭi, kaṉṉu (kaṉṟu), kālikaḷ, kāḷa (kāḷi), kutira (kutiri), kuraṅṅu (kuraṅku), nari, nāy, paṉṉi (paṉṟi), puli, pūcca (pūci = pūṉi), māṉ, muyalu, mūri, kuṟukkaṉ (kuṟunari).
6. paṟavip peyar
īcca (ī), kaḻuka (kaḻuku), kākka (kākki), kuyil, kūmaṉ (kūki), kokku, ceṅṅāli (ceṅkāli=puṟā), tārāvu (tārā), paruntu, piṭakkōḻi (peṭṭik kōḻi), prāvu (puṟā), mayil, vaṇṭu, vāval, tatta (tatti  = kiḷi), kiḷi.
7. ūrvaṉavaṟṟiṉ peyar
irimpu (eṟumpu), ōntu (ōnti), eli, kīri, cuṇṭeli, tēḷ, pāmpu, puḻu, ñāṅkūḻ, cital.
8. nīr vāḻvaṉavaṟṟiṉ peyar
ñāṇṭu (naṇṭu),  tavaḷa (tavaḷi), mīṉ, ayila (ayiri), āral, mutala (mutali) mutaliyaṉa.
9. marañceṭip peyar
avara (avari), āl, iñci, uḻuṉṉu (uḻuntu), eḷ, ēlam, karumuḷaku, kīra (kīri), ceṭi, ceṟu payaṟu (ciṟupayaṟu), cēmpu, cēṉa (cēṉi) teṅku,  nel, paṭōl (puṭali), payaṟu, parutti, paṉa         (paṉi), pilāvu (palā), pul, puḷi, pukiyila (pukiyili) maram,         maññaḷ (mañcaḷ), mā, mutira (mutiri = kāṇam), muḷḷaṅki, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy ( = kattarikkāy), vāḻa (vāḻi), veṇṭakki (veṇṭikkāy), veḷḷari, veṟṟila (veṟṟili), paṭṭāṇip payaṟu, uruḷakkiḻaṅku, muntiriṅṅa (muntiriki), ñāṟu (nāṟṟu), kaḷa (kaḷi), cūral = pirampu), vēmpu, ñāṇal, ñāval (nāval) mutaliyaṉa.
10.  karuvippeyar
aram, āṇi, īrccavāḷ, uralu, ulakka (ulakki),  ēttam (ēṟṟam), uḷi, katti, kattiri, karaṇṭi, kōṭṭu, (koṭṭu), kōṭāli, cakku (cekku), cāṭṭa (cāṭṭi), cuttika (cuttiki), cuḻikkuṟṟi, cūlam, tuṭuppu, turutti, nukam, maḻu, muḷ, mūlamaṭṭam, vāḷ, vil, kuṇṭal (tūṇṭil), naṅkūram mutaliyaṉa.
11. aimpūtap peyar
nilam, veḷḷam, tī, kāṟṟu, viṇṇu.
12. ulōkap peyar
irimpu (irumpu), īyam, urukku, cempu, takaram, tuttanākam, piccaḷa (pittaṉi), poṉ, vajram (vayiram), veḷḷi.
13. ūrtip peyar
ōṭi, ōṭam, kappal, tēr, tōṇi, paṭaku, vaṇṭi mutaliyaṉa.
14, uṇavup peyar
ari (arici), appam, ira (iri), iṟacci (iṟicci), uppu, uṟa (uṟi mōr), ūṇ, eṇṇa (eṇṇey), kaññi (kañci), kaṭuku, kaṟi, cōṟu, tayir, taviṭu, tīṉ, tēṉ, ti (ney), palahāram (palakāram), pāl, macāla (macāli), maruṉṉu (maruntu), muṭṭa (muṭṭi), muḷaku taṇṇi (miḷaku taṇṇīr), mōr, veṇṇa (veṇṇey) mutaliyaṉa.
15. āṭiyaṇip peyar
uṭuppu, kampiḷi (kampaḷi), kāl caṭṭa, kāluṟa (kāluṟi), kuppāyam, kuṭutta (kuṭittuṇi), cilampu, cerippu (ceruppu), talakkeṭṭu, talappāvu (talippāki), tuṇi, tuvāla (tuvāli), paṭṭu, pāvāṭa (pāvāṭi), puṭava (puṭavi), mōtiram, mūkkutti, vaḷa (vaḷi) mutaliyaṉa.
16. taṭṭumuṭṭup peyar
aṇa (aṇi), kayiṟu, kalam, kiṇṭi (keṇṭi), kuṭa (kuṭi), kaṭṭi, cākku, toṭṭi, palaka (palaki), pāy, mēca (mēci), viḷakku, cīppu, kaṇṇāṭi, pustakam (pottakam), kaṭiyāram, taṭṭu, kiṇṇam, cempu, pokkaṇam, peṭṭi, muṟam mutaliyaṉa.
17. iṭap peyar
akam, aṅṅāṭi (aṅkāṭi), iṭa (iṭam), iṟa (iṟavu), ula (uli), eḻuttuppaḷḷi (paḷḷikkūṭam), kaṭal, kara (kari), kallaṟa (kallaṟi), kāṭu, kiṇaṟu, kiḻakku, kuṭi (= vīṭu), kuṭil, kuḻi, kuḷam, kōṭṭa (kōṭṭi), cuvar, tīvu, tuṟamukam (tuṟimukam), tekku,  teru, tōṭṭam, nāṭu, paṭṭaṇam, pāṟa (pāṟi), puṟam, mala (mali), matilu, māḷika (māḷiki), muṟṟam, mūla (mūli), lōkam (ulakam), vaṭakku, vayal, vaḻi, vātil (vācal), vāṉam, vīṭu, kuṇṭu, (aṟi), cēri, illam, kaḻaṉi, kēṇi.
18. kālap peyar

21. kuṇappeyar

22. nōyp peyar
kāccal (kāyccal), kuṭalēṟṟam, kuṭaccal (kuṭiccal), kuru, cūla (cūli), pakkavātam mutaliyaṉa.

eṇṇaṭi uyartiṇip peyar : oruvar, iruvar, mūvar, nālvar, aivar, aṟuvar, eḻuvar, pantiruvar (paṉṉiruvar), patiṉālvar, nūṟṟuvar mutaliyaṉa.
24. toḻiṟ peyar
(1)	mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar; aṭi, piṭi, veṭṭu, kaḷi, paṇa.
(2)	mutaṉili tirinta toḻiṟpeyar: ūṇ, tīṉ, cūṭu, pōr.
(3)	mutaṉili tirintu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar;
	tavi-tākam, paṭi-pāṭam;
(4)	mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar;
        āṭu-āṭṭu, pāṭu-pāṭṭu.
(5)	mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭittu vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar:  ēṟu - ēṟṟam, ōṭu - ōṭṭam.
(6)	vikutipeṟṟa peyar
vikuti	toḻiṟpeyar	vikuti	toḻiṟpeyar
a(ai)	paka(paki)	pu	piṟappu
al	tuppal	vu(u)	varavu
 	viṟayal (viṟiyal)	ka(ki)	ceyka
cal	pāccal(pāyccal)
tam	piṭittal	vi	kēḷvi
ti	poṟuti
(7)	kālaṅkāṭṭun toḻiṟpeyar:  ceytatu, ceyyuṉṉatu, ceyvatu.
(8)	etirmaṟit toḻiṟpeyar: ceyyāyka, aṟiyāyka.
(9)	pakuti vaḻakkaṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar:  caṇṭi.
viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar
terinili:	ceytavaṉ, ceyyuṉṉavaṉ, ceyvōṉ mutaliyaṉa.
kuṟippu:	valiyavaṉ, ceṟiyavaṉ, paḻiyatu, putiyatu mutaliyaṉa.
(ii) viṉiccoṟkaḷ
cila mukkiya viṉikaḷ
akal, añcu, aṭi, aṭe (aṭi), amiḻ, ari,

kuṟippu :	maliyāḷa viṉikaḷellām ēvalākum pōtu perumpālum viyaṅkōḷ vaṭivu koḷḷum.
e-ṭu: cey-ceyyuka, tā-tarika.

āku eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi
iṟanta kālam:	ñāṉ āyi	=	nāṉ āṉēṉ.
 	nī āyi	=	nī āṉāy?
 	avaṉ āyi	=	avaṉ āṉāṉ.
nikaḻ kālam:	ñāṉ ākuṉṉu	=	nāṉ irukkiṟēṉ.
 	nī ākuṉṉu	=	nī irukkiṟāy.
 	avaṉ ākuṉṉu	=	avaṉ irukkiṟāṉ.
etir kālam:	ñāṉ ākum	=	nāṉ iruppēṉ.
 	nī ākum	=	nī iruppāy.
 	avaṉ ākum	=	avaṉ iruppāṉ.
iru eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi (kalavikkālam) 
i.kā.	ñāṉ ceytiruṉṉu	=	nāṉ ceytiruntēṉ
	nī ceytiruṉṉu	=	nī ceyntiruntāy.
	avaṉ ceytiruṉṉu	=	avaṉ ceytiruntāṉ
ni.kā.	ñāṉ ceytirukkuṉṉu	=	nāṉ ceytirukkiṟēṉ.
	nī ceytirukkuṉṉu	=	nī ceytirukkiṟāy.
	avaṉ ceytirukkuṉṉu	=	avaṉ ceytirukkiṟāṉ.
e.kā.	ñāṉ ceytirukkum	=	nāṉ ceytiruppēṉ.
	nī ceytirukkum	=	nī ceytiruppāy.
	ava ceytirukkum	=	avaṉ ceytiruppāṉ.
uṇṭu illi eṉṉum tuṇiviṉikaḷ
eṉikkuṇṭu	=	eṉakkuṇṭu	eṉikkilla	=	eṉakkilli
niṉakkuṇṭu	=	uṉakkuṇṭu	niṉakkilla	=	uṉakkilli
avaṉuṇṭu	=	avaṉukkuṇṭu	avaṉikkilla	=	avaṉukkilli
alla eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi
ñāṉ alla	=	nāṉ allēṉ
nī alla	=	nī alli
avaṉ alla	=	avaṉ allaṉ
kuṟippu :	tamiḻilum ulaka vaḻakkil alla eṉṉum palaviṉpāl vaṭivam vaḻuvāka irutiṇi yimpāl mūviṭaṅkaṭkum potuvāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
cila puṇarviṉikaḷ
aṭuttuvā, uṇṭāku, kāttiru, koṇṭuvā,  paṟaññūvekka, vittiru, veḷippeṭu, koṇṭupō, tāṇupōka.
taṉviṉi piṟaviṉiyākum vaḻikaḷ
	(1)	pakuti vali yiraṭṭal - e-ṭu: uruku - urukku,  uṇṭāku - uṇṭākku, māṟu - māṟṟu.
	(2)	pakuti (meli) valittal - e-ṭu: kalaṅṅu - kalakku.
	(3)	vikuti peṟutal - e-ṭu: kāṇ-kāṇi (kāṇpi) nīḷ-     nīṭṭu, kaṭi - kaṭippi, cuma - cumattu, kāy - kāccu (kāyccu).
kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷ
valiya (periya), nalla, ceṟiya (ciṟiya), paḻaya (paḻiya), putiya, cila, pala mutaliyaṉa.
kuṟippu :	viṉi yeccaṅkaḷ- naṉṉāyi,  muḻuvaṉ, vēkam,  mutaliyaṉa.

toṭarccoṟkaḷ

(oru pakkam cāyka), peṇṇiṉe vāḻikka (vāḻvikka),  puṟattāka vaṇṭikeṭṭu, tīṉpaṇṭam, eṉṉeṉṉēkkum (eṉṟeṉṟikkum),  piṟavik kuruṭaṉ,  pāl kāccu,  iṭaviṭāte,  kaḷa  paṟi,  tīppeṭṭi mutaliyaṉa.
pakirvup peyar
ōrōṉṉu (ovvoṉṟu), īraṇṭu (ivviraṇṭu), 
mummūṉṉu, naṉṉālu, ayyañcu, patuppattu (pappattu).
maraput toṭarkaḷ
akavum puṟavum nōkka, avaṉ tulaññu pōyi, atiṉṟe ippuṟavum appuṟavum nōkkātē, uruṭṭum piraṭṭum paṟaka, pottip potiññu vikka, eṉikku entu pōyi, kaṭikaṭi eṉṉu paṟaka, kaṇakkum kāryavum, kaṇṇu miḻikkuṉṉatiṉ mumpē, karakaṇṭavaṉ, mukam muṟiccu paṟaka, kaḷavum kayyumā yakappaṭuka, orunāḷ muṅkūṭṭi, kāṭu vāvā vīṭu pōpō eṉṉu paṟayuṉṉa kālam, kāṇiccuk koṭukka, niṉṟe mukattu mica muḷac ciṭṭillē, avaṉṟē kāryam kaḻiññu, itil kāryam oṉṉumilla, maṟṟoruttaṉ collappaṭṭikku naṭakkuṉṉavaṉ; raṇṭum keṭṭa, oru kiyāyirukka, kikaṇṭa, veṭṭā veḷiccam, cūcimuṉaye koṇṭu kuttu vāṉuḷḷa nilam koṭukkuṉṉilla, ūrum uṭalum illa, eṉṟe vīṭṭil eccilum kuppayum āyi, ivaṉ oru āṇi ākuṉṉu, oṉṉum iṟaṅṅāta camayam, untum taḷḷam (aṭipiṭi), nañcu tiṉṉapōlē āyi, ēnti ēntikkoṇṭu naṭakka, kaṇṇima kūṭṭiyilla, untalum piṭiyumāka, karakāṇuka, uṭal ompatukāṇum viṟekka, raṇṭu kiyilum ciraṭṭapiṭiccu pōka.
vākkiyaṅkaḷ (coṟṟoṭarkaḷ)
ñāṉ ā āḷōṭu ī kutiraye vāṅki = nāṉ av āḷiṭam ik kutiriyi vāṅkiṉēṉ.
accaṉ puṟappeṭṭu pōya cēṣam oruttaṉ vannu eṉṟe cōṟu tiṉṉu = attaṉ (appaṉ) puṟappaṭṭup pōṉapiṉpu oruttaṉ vantu eṉṉuṭiya cōṟṟit tiṉṟāṉ. 
eṉṟē cerippu veṭippākkeṇam eṉṉu ñāṉ tivasan tōṟum kalpikkuṉṉeṉkilum, nī itu orikkalum naṉṉāyi ceyyāṟilla = eṉṉuṭiya ceruppit tuppuravākka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nāṉ nāḷtōṟum coṉṉālum nī iti orukālum naṉṟāyc ceykiṟatilli.
paḻamoḻikaḷ (paḻañcol - ma.)
akattiṭṭāl puṟattaṟiyām.
ari (arici) eṟiññāl āyiram kākka.
aḷavu kaṭaṉṉāl amrutum nañcu.
ākum kālam ceytatu cākum kālam kāṇām.
āyiram mākāṇi aṟupattu raṇṭara.
ikkaraṉiṉṉu nōkkumpōl akkara pacca.
irimpūra kallum tēyum.
īr eṭuttāl pēṉ kūliyō.
kaṭalil kāyam kalakkiyatu pōle.
kāmattiṉṉu kaṇṇilla.
kāṟṟuḷḷa pōḷ tūṟṟaṇum.
kāryattiṉṉu kaḻuta kālum piṭikkēṇam.
kuraṅṅiṉṟe kayyil pūmāla kiṭṭiyatupōle.
kurakkuṉṉa nāyi kaṭikkayilla.
kōṭāte kāṇi koṭuttāl kōṭi koṭutta palam.
kōṭi kōṭi koṭuttāl kāṇi koṭutta palam.
tāḻē koytavaṉ ēṟē cumakkēṇam.
tīyillāte pukayuṇṭāka yilla.
nari nareccālum kaṭikkum.
nilāviṉe nōkki nāyi kurakkumpōle.
palatuḷḷi peru veḷḷam.
marattiṉṉu kāyi kaṉamō?
maruṉṉum viruṉṉum mūṉṉu nāḷ.
malarṉṉu kiṭaṉṉu tuppiyāl māṟattu viḻum.
mīṅkaṇṭam vēṇṭāta pūccayilla.
raṇṭu tōṇiyil kāl veccutu pōle.
	     tāḻa = aṭiyil. ēṟa = mikutiyāka.
oru kati
oru akrahārattil oru pirāmaṇaṉ pārttiruṉṉu. avaṉṉu makaṉ oṉṉē uṇṭāyiruṉṉu. oru nāḷ avaṉ oru vaḻikku yātra puṟappeṭṭu, makaṉeyum  kūṭṭikkoṇṭu pōyi, vaḻiyil veccu ā ceṟukkaṉ accaṉe viḷiccu, ayyō! puli varuṉṉu eṉṉu uṟakkē nilaviḷiccu. accaṉ tiriññu nōkkiyapōḷ puliye kaṇṭilla. avar piṉṉēyum kuṟaya tūram pōyapōḷ vāstavattil oru puli vaṉṉu ceṟukkaṉe piṭiccu, ā ceṟukkaṉ vīṇṭum accaṉe viḷiccu, taṉṟe makaṉ mumpēpōlē kaḷivākka paṟayuṉṉu eṉṉu vicāriccu tiriññu nōkkiyilla. puli ceṟukkaṉe koṉṉu tiṉṉu.
itaṉ coṉmoḻipeyarppu
oru pārppaṉac cēriyil oru pārppāṉ vatintiruntāṉ. avaṉukku makaṉ oruvaṉē uṇṭāyiruntāṉ. oru nāḷ avaṉ oru vaḻippōkkup puṟappaṭṭu makaṉiyuṅ kūṭṭikkoṇṭu pōṉāṉ. vaḻiyil vittu ac ciṟukkaṉ (ciṟuvaṉ) attaṉi (appaṉi) viḷittu, aiyō! puli varukiṟatu eṉṟu urakkak kattiṉāṉ. attaṉ tirumpi nōkkiya pōḻtu puliyik kaṇṭilli. avarkaḷ piṉṉēyum kuṟinta tolivu pōya pōḻtu uṇmiyil oru puli vantu ciṟukkaṉip piṭittatu. ac ciṟukkaṉ mīṇṭum attaṉi viḷittāṉ. taṉṉuṭiya makaṉ muṉpēpōla viḷiyāṭṭukkuc colkiṟāṉ eṉṟu eṇṇit tirumpip pārkkavilli. puli ciṟukkaṉik koṉṟu tiṉṟatu.
kuṟippu :	ciṟukkaṉ (ā. pā.) - ciṟukki (pe. pā.). tamiḻil ciṟukkaṉ ceyyuḷ vaḻakku; ciṟukki ulaka vaḻakku - kaḷi (ma.) viḷiyāṭṭu  (ta.).
2
kaṉṉaṭam
kaṉṉaṭam eṉṉum peyar
maliyāḷattiṟku aṭuttut tamiḻōṭu toṭarpuḷḷatu kaṉṉaṭam. kaṉṉaṭam eṉpatu karunaṭam eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripu. itu mutalāvatu kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭik kuṟittup piṉpu aṅku vaḻaṅkum moḻiyik kuṟittatu. itaṉ paḻiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ karunāṭu, karunāṭakam eṉpaṉa. kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭār karunāṭar eṉṟum, karunaṭar eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭār.
karunaṭam allatu karunāṭakam eṉṉum collukku iruporuḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi (1) kariya nāṭu, 
(2) karuṅkūttu eṉpaṉa.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟ perumpakuti karical nilamāyiruppatāl, kariya nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭaṉar kuṇṭeṭ paṇṭitarum (Dr. Gundert) kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyārum.
kūttukaḷil iḻinta vakikkuk karuṅkūttu eṉṟu peyar.
mutupārppāṉ vīḻkkip peruṅkaruṅ kūttu(kalit. 65 :29)
 naṭam = kūttu. naṭaṉ = kūttaṉ.
vaḷinaṭaṉ melliṇarp pūṅkoṭi mēvara nuṭaṅka 
(paripā. 22 : 42)
 naṭar = kūttar.
viṭarum tūrttarum naṭarum uḷḷiṭṭa 	 (kuṟaḷ. parimē. uri)
nāṭakam = kati taḻuvivarum kūttu. mikap paḻimiyāṉa          anākarika allatu kaṇmūṭip paḻakkattip `paḻiya karunāṭakam          eṉpar; iṅkuk `karunāṭakam eṉpatu paḻimiyāṉa anākarikattik kuṟikkalām. ākavē, karunaṭam allatu karunāṭakam eṉṉum peyar karuṅkūttu nikaḻum nāṭu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭatāyirukkalām. cilappatikārattil karunāṭar kuṟikkappaṭum pōtellām tiruntāmiyik kuṟikkum `koṭu eṉṉum aṭi koṭuttē kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
koṅkaṇar kaliṅkar koṭuṅkaru nāṭar	(cilap. 25:156)
koṅkaṇak kūttaruṅ koṭuṅkaru nāṭarum     (cilap. 26:106)
iṉṟum yakṣakāṉam eṉṉum karuṅkūttu kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil naṭikkappaṭṭu varuvatākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āyiṉum karunaṭarik karunāṭar eṉṉum vaḻakkum uṇmiyāṉum, kūttākiya kāraṇattiṉum nilavakiyākiya kāraṇam peyarppēṟṟiṟkuc ciṟattalāṉum, karicaṟpāṅkāṉa nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvatē poruttamām. kari nāṭu eṉpatu karunāṭu eṉa maruviṟṟeṉpar cilar.
kaṉṉaṭanāṭ ṭelli
teṟkē nīlamali (nīlakiri) yiliruntu vaṭakkē pīṭārp (Bidar) pakuti vari. micūr kaṉṉaṭam teṉmarāṭṭam (makārāṣṭiram) aitarāpāttiṉ teṉmēṟkup pakuti ākiya cīmikaḷiṟ perumpālum tāymoḻiyākap pēcappaṭuvatu kaṉṉaṭam.
nīlamaliyiluḷḷa paṭakar (vaṭakar), eṉṉum malivāṇar kaṉṉaṭattiṉ tiripāṉa oru moḻiyip pēcukiṉṟaṉar.
16ām nūṟṟāṇṭil teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vanta makamatiya maṉṉar, aṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉmēṟ karunaṭa maṉṉar āṇi celuttiyami kāraṇamākat tamiḻnāṭṭiyum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭōṭu cērttuk karnāṭṭak (karunāṭakam) eṉṟaṉar.  ati āṅkilēyar karnāṭṭik (Carnatic) eṉṟu tirittaṉar.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭu varalāṟu
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiṟ ciṟanta pakuti micūrc cīmiyākum. micūril taṟpōtu haḷepīṭu (Halebid) eṉa vaḻaṅkum tuvari nakā (tuvāra camuttiram) ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarc centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērntatāyiruntatu. itu tuvarāpati eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, akattiyaṉār............ tuvarāpatip         pōntu nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇal vaḻikkaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmariyum patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷiruḷḷiṭṭāriyum aruvāḷariyuṅ koṇṭu pōntu kāṭukeṭuttu nāṭākki eṉat tolkāppiyap pāyiravuriyilum, maliya mātavaṉ nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇaluḻi narapatiyaruṭaṉ koṇarnta patiṉeṇvakik kuṭippiṟanta vēḷirkkum eṉa akattiṇiyiyal 32 ām nūṟpāvuriyilum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
paṇṭic cēranāṭṭil micūrc cīmiyiṉ teṉ pakutiyum cērntiruntatu.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil micūrt tuvarinakari āṇṭavaṉ iruṅkōvēḷ eṉṉum tamiḻac ciṟṟaracaṉ. avaṉ vaṭapakkattil oru muṉivariṉ ōma kuṇṭattil tōṉṟit tuvari nakari nāṟpattoṉpatu talimuṟiyākat toṉṟutoṭṭu āṇṭuvanta vēḷirkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ eṉṟum, oru muṉivar tavañceytu koṇṭirukkiyil avarkku iṭiyūṟu ceyyavanta oru puliyi avar ēvaṟpaṭi koṉṟamiyāl pulikaṭimāl eṉappaṭṭā ṉeṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvaṉ.
nīyē, vaṭapāṉ muṉivaṉ taṭaviṉuṭ ṭōṉṟic
 cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici

eṉṟu kapilar pāṭutal kāṇka.
ip pāṭṭiṉ aṭikkuṟippil, tapaṅkareṉṉu muṉivar         oru kāṭṭil tavañceykiyil oru puli avarmēṟ pāytaṟku neruṅka, atukaṇṭa am muṉivar aṅku vēṭṭiyāṭi vanta caḷaṉeṉṉum yātava aracaṉi nōkki `hoy saḷa eṉṟu         kūṟa, avaṉ ap puliyit taṉ ampāl eytu kaṭintamiyāl hoysaḷaṉeṉṟum pulikaṭimāleṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭā           ṉeṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar; caḷakapurattiyaṭutta kāṭṭiluḷḷa taṉ kulatēvatiyāṉa vāsattikā tēviyic caḷaṉeṉṉum aracaṉ vaṇaṅkac ceṉṟapōtu puliyāl taṭukkappaṭṭu varuntukiyil, ak kōyililirunta periyavar avaṉi nōkki `hos saḷa eṉṟu kūṟi ōr irumput taṭiyi aruḷa, avaṉ atukoṇṭu ataṉik koṉṟamipaṟṟi, `hoy saḷaṉ eṉṟum `pulikaṭimāl eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉaṉeṉṟu vēṟu cilar kūṟuvar eṉṟu cāminātiyar avarkaḷ varintuḷḷaṉar.
piṟkālattil 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tuvāracamuttirattil (Halebid) niṟuvappaṭṭa hoycaḷa pallāḷa marapu kaṭikkaḻakak kālap pulikaṭimāliṉ vaḻiyatē. pallāḷaṉ eṉṉum peyar vallāḷaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripu. va-pa, pōli, 
o, nō: vaṇṭi - paṇṭi, vaku-paku, vallāḷaṉ = valiya āṇmiyi yuṭiyavaṉ. oru maṟavaṉuṭiya illiyum ūriyum iyalpiyum colli avaṉ āṇmit taṉmiyi mikuttuk kūṟum puṟattuṟikku vallāṇmulli (pu.ve. 177) eṉṟu peyar.

eṉṟār akattiyaṉār eṉpatu naṉṉūl mayilinātar 
uri. (pa. 161). iv akattiya nūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭum peyarkaḷ mutalāvatu nāṭṭik kuṟittavi yeṉṟum piṉpu moḻi tirintapiṉ moḻiyik kuṟittaṉaveṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
cēranāṭu kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē kuṭa (mēṟkut toṭarcci) malikku mēṟpāl vēṟum kīḻppāl vēṟumākap pirintu pōyiṟṟu. kīḻppāl nāṭu mīṇṭum teṟkil koṅku nāṭum vaṭakkil kaṅkanāṭum iṭiyil atikināṭu tuvarināṭu mutaliyaṉavumākap pirintuviṭṭatu. atikināṭu takaṭūri (iṟṟit tarmapuriyi)t talinakarākak koṇṭu atikamāṉ marapiṉar āṇṭu vantatu. kaṅkanāṭu, ataṉ vaṭakkil kaṅkamarapiṉar kuvaḷālapuratti (kōlār)t talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭu vantatu. itu kaṅkapāṭi eṉṟu kalveṭṭukaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
veṅki nāṭuṅ kaṅka pāṭiyum 	(S.S.I. I. 94)
kaṅka marapiṉarāṉa ciṟṟaracar kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilē, maṟattiṟ ciṟantu peyar peṟṟavarāyiruntaṉar.

eṉap paḻiya nūlkaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka.
ik kaṅka marapic cērntavaṉē, 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ kālattaṉum, amarāparaṇaṉ srīmat kuvaḷālapura paramēcuvaraṉ, kaṅkakulōṟpavaṉ eṉṟu taṉ meykkīrttikaḷiṟ pārāṭṭappeṟupavaṉum, pavaṇanti muṉivarikkoṇṭu naṉṉūli ākkuvittavaṉumākiya cīyakaṅkaṉ eṉpavaṉ. ivaṉ oru tamiḻilakkaṇatti iyaṟṟuvittatiṉālum, naṉṉūṟ ciṟappuppāyiram,
kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭam
 eṉunāṉ kelliyiṉ iruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ
